import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Check, Edit3, Eye, EyeOff, MapPin, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { DirectoryEntry, DirectoryGroup, Language, PlantLocation } from '../../types';

const LANGS: Language[] = ['ru', 'be', 'en', 'zh'];

const createEmptyEntry = (group: DirectoryGroup = 'departments'): DirectoryEntry => ({
    id: `contact-${Date.now().toString(36)}`,
                                                                                     group,
                                                                                     visible: true,
                                                                                     title: { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' },
                                                                                     person: { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' },
                                                                                     address: { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' },
                                                                                     note: { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' },
                                                                                     phones: [{ number: '+375 (152) ' }],
                                                                                     emails: [{ address: 'info@milk.by' }],
                                                                                     website: '',
});

export const AdminContactsTab: React.FC = () => {
    const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
    const {
        siteSettings,
        updateSiteSettings,
        directoryEntries,
        addDirectoryEntry,
        updateDirectoryEntry,
        toggleDirectoryEntryVisibility,
        deleteDirectoryEntry,
        locations,
        updateLocation,
    } = useCatalogStore();

    const [editLang, setEditLang] = useState<Language>(language);
    const [groupFilter, setGroupFilter] = useState<'all' | DirectoryGroup>('all');
    const [editingEntry, setEditingEntry] = useState<DirectoryEntry | null>(null);
    const [creatingEntry, setCreatingEntry] = useState<DirectoryEntry | null>(null);
    const [editingLocation, setEditingLocation] = useState<PlantLocation | null>(null);

    const { contactMeta } = siteSettings;

    const copy = {
        ru: {
            heading: 'Контакты, реквизиты и площадки',
            globalBlock: 'Основные контакты компании (шапки Контактов, Экспорта, Партнёров и Футер)',
            hqAddress: 'Юридический адрес',
            unp: 'УНП',
            schedule: 'График работы',
            citizenHours: 'Приём граждан',
            receptionCard: 'Приёмная',
            salesCard: 'Отдел продаж и логистики',
            exportCard: 'Отдел ВЭД и экспорта',
            dirTitle: 'Справочник подразделений, филиалов и дилеров',
            addContact: 'Добавить контакт',
            groups: {
                all: 'Все разделы',
                sales: 'Продажи и дилеры',
                management: 'Руководство',
                departments: 'Отделы и службы',
                branches: 'Филиалы и хозяйства',
            },
            locationsTitle: 'Площадки на карте главной страницы',
            save: 'Сохранить',
            cancel: 'Отмена',
        },
        be: {
            heading: 'Кантакты, рэквізіты і пляцоўкі',
            globalBlock: 'Асноўныя кантакты кампаніі (Кантакты, Экспарт, Партнёрам і Футэр)',
            hqAddress: 'Юрыдычны адрас',
            unp: 'УНП',
            schedule: 'Графік працы',
            citizenHours: 'Прыём грамадзян',
            receptionCard: 'Прыёмная',
            salesCard: 'Аддзел продажаў і лагістыкі',
            exportCard: 'Аддзел ЗЭД і экспарту',
            dirTitle: 'Даведнік падраздзяленняў, філіялаў і дылераў',
            addContact: 'Дадаць кантакт',
            groups: {
                all: 'Усе раздзелы',
                sales: 'Продажы і дылеры',
                management: 'Кіраўніцтва',
                departments: 'Аддзелы і службы',
                branches: 'Філіялы і гаспадаркі',
            },
            locationsTitle: 'Пляцоўкі на карце галоўнай старонкі',
            save: 'Захаваць',
            cancel: 'Адмена',
        },
        en: {
            heading: 'Company Contacts, Directory & Map Sites',
            globalBlock: 'Primary Company Channels (Contacts, Export, Partners, B2B Modal & Footer)',
            hqAddress: 'Headquarters Address',
            unp: 'Tax ID (UNP)',
            schedule: 'Working Hours',
            citizenHours: 'Public Reception',
            receptionCard: 'Reception Desk',
            salesCard: 'Sales & Logistics',
            exportCard: 'Export / VED',
            dirTitle: 'Departments, Branches & Dealers Directory',
            addContact: 'Add Contact Entry',
            groups: {
                all: 'All Groups',
                sales: 'Sales & Dealers',
                management: 'Leadership',
                departments: 'Departments',
                branches: 'Branches & Farms',
            },
            locationsTitle: 'Homepage Interactive Map Locations',
            save: 'Save',
            cancel: 'Cancel',
        },
        zh: {
            heading: '联系方式、企业信息与生产基地地图',
            globalBlock: '核心联系方式（同步更新联系页面、出口专区、合作专区及页脚）',
            hqAddress: '总部地址',
            unp: '税号 (UNP)',
            schedule: '工作时间',
            citizenHours: '公众接待时间',
            receptionCard: '总机前台',
            salesCard: '销售与物流部',
            exportCard: '国际出口外贸部',
            dirTitle: '部门、分公司与官方经销商通讯录',
            addContact: '新增联系条目',
            groups: {
                all: '全部分组',
                sales: '销售与经销商',
                management: '公司管理层',
                departments: '职能部门',
                branches: '分公司与牧场',
            },
            locationsTitle: '首页交互地图生产基地',
            save: '保存',
            cancel: '取消',
        },
    }[language];

    const visibleEntries = directoryEntries.filter(
        (item) => groupFilter === 'all' || item.group === groupFilter
    );

    const renderEntryForm = (
        draft: DirectoryEntry,
        setDraft: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<DirectoryEntry | null>>,
        isNew: boolean
    ) => (
        <form
        onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (!(draft.title.ru || draft.title.en || draft.title[editLang]).trim()) return;
            const cleaned: DirectoryEntry = {
                ...draft,
                phones: draft.phones.filter((p) => p.number.trim()),
          emails: (draft.emails || []).filter((em) => em.address.trim()),
            };
            if (isNew) {
                addDirectoryEntry(cleaned);
            } else {
                updateDirectoryEntry(cleaned);
            }
            setDraft(null);
        }}
        className="border border-[#0057B8]/40 rounded-2xl p-5 bg-blue-50/40 space-y-4"
        >
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h4 className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
        {isNew ? copy.addContact : localize(draft.title)}
        </h4>
        <div className="inline-flex bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-0.5">
        {LANGS.map((l) => (
            <button
            key={l}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditLang(l)}
            className={`px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold rounded ${
                editLang === l ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
            >
            {l.toUpperCase()}
            </button>
        ))}
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Раздел</label>
        <select
        value={draft.group}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev ? { ...prev, group: e.target.value as DirectoryGroup } : null
            )
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-2.5 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        >
        <option value="sales">{copy.groups.sales}</option>
        <option value="management">{copy.groups.management}</option>
        <option value="departments">{copy.groups.departments}</option>
        <option value="branches">{copy.groups.branches}</option>
        </select>
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        Название отдела / карточки ({editLang.toUpperCase()}) *
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        required={editLang === 'ru'}
        value={draft.title[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? { ...prev, title: { ...prev.title, [editLang]: e.target.value } }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        Руководитель / ФИО ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={draft.person?.[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? {
                ...prev,
                person: {
                    ru: prev.person?.ru || '',
                    be: prev.person?.be || '',
                    en: prev.person?.en || '',
                    zh: prev.person?.zh || '',
                    [editLang]: e.target.value,
                },
            }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>

        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        Адрес ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={draft.address?.[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? {
                ...prev,
                address: {
                    ru: prev.address?.ru || '',
                    be: prev.address?.be || '',
                    en: prev.address?.en || '',
                    zh: prev.address?.zh || '',
                    [editLang]: e.target.value,
                },
            }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        Примечание ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={draft.note?.[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? {
                ...prev,
                note: {
                    ru: prev.note?.ru || '',
                    be: prev.note?.be || '',
                    en: prev.note?.en || '',
                    zh: prev.note?.zh || '',
                    [editLang]: e.target.value,
                },
            }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Website</label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={draft.website || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            setDraft((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, website: e.target.value } : null))
        }
        placeholder="https://..."
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm font-mono bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>

        {/* Dynamic Phones List */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">Телефоны</span>
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? { ...prev, phones: [...prev.phones, { number: '+375 ' }] }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:underline"
        >
        + Добавить телефон
        </button>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
        {draft.phones.map((ph, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="flex flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-2">
            <input
            type="text"
            value={ph.label?.[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                setDraft((prev) => {
                    if (!prev) return null;
                    const phones = [...prev.phones];
                    const curLabel = phones[idx].label || { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' };
                    phones[idx] = {
                        ...phones[idx],
                        label: val ? { ...curLabel, [editLang]: val } : undefined,
                    };
                    return { ...prev, phones };
                });
            }}
            placeholder="Метка (A1, МТС, Факс…)"
            className="w-36 h-8 px-2.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
            type="text"
            value={ph.number}
            onChange={(e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                setDraft((prev) => {
                    if (!prev) return null;
                    const phones = [...prev.phones];
                    phones[idx] = { ...phones[idx], number: val };
                    return { ...prev, phones };
                });
            }}
            placeholder="+375 (152) ..."
            className="flex-1 h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
                setDraft((prev) =>
                prev
                ? { ...prev, phones: prev.phones.filter((_, i) => i !== idx) }
                : null
                )
            }
            className="px-2 text-xs text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg"
            >
            ✕
            </button>
            </div>
        ))}
        </div>
        </div>

        {/* Dynamic Emails List */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">E-mail адреса</span>
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() =>
            setDraft((prev) =>
            prev
            ? { ...prev, emails: [...(prev.emails || []), { address: '' }] }
            : null
            )
        }
        className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:underline"
        >
        + Добавить e-mail
        </button>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
        {(draft.emails || []).map((em, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="flex flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap gap-2">
            <input
            type="text"
            value={em.label || ''}
            onChange={(e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                setDraft((prev) => {
                    if (!prev) return null;
                    const emails = [...(prev.emails || [])];
                    emails[idx] = { ...emails[idx], label: val || undefined };
                    return { ...prev, emails };
                });
            }}
            placeholder="Метка (Заявки…)"
            className="w-36 h-8 px-2.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
            type="email"
            value={em.address}
            onChange={(e) => {
                const val = e.target.value;
                setDraft((prev) => {
                    if (!prev) return null;
                    const emails = [...(prev.emails || [])];
                    emails[idx] = { ...emails[idx], address: val };
                    return { ...prev, emails };
                });
            }}
            placeholder="name@milk.by"
            className="flex-1 h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
                setDraft((prev) =>
                prev
                ? {
                    ...prev,
                    emails: (prev.emails || []).filter((_, i) => i !== idx),
                }
                : null
                )
            }
            className="px-2 text-xs text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg"
            >
            ✕
            </button>
            </div>
        ))}
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setDraft(null)}
        className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        >
        {copy.cancel}
        </button>
        <button
        type="submit"
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg"
        >
        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>{copy.save}</span>
        </button>
        </div>
        </form>
    );

    return (
        <div className="space-y-8">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{copy.heading}</h2>
        <div className="inline-flex bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-0.5">
        {LANGS.map((l) => (
            <button
            key={l}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditLang(l)}
            className={`px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded ${
                editLang === l ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white' : 'text-slate-600'
            }`}
            >
            {l.toUpperCase()}
            </button>
        ))}
        </div>
        </div>

        {/* 1. Global Contacts & HQ Requisites */}
        <section className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 bg-slate-50/70 space-y-5">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900 border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">
        {copy.globalBlock}
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.hqAddress} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.hqAddress[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    hqAddress: { ...prev.contactMeta.hqAddress, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.unp}
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.unp}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, unp: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm font-mono bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.schedule} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.schedule[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    schedule: { ...prev.contactMeta.schedule, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        <div className="sm:col-span-2">
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.citizenHours} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.citizenHours[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    citizenHours: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.citizenHours,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {/* Reception */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0057B8] uppercase">
        {copy.receptionCard}
        </div>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.receptionTitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    receptionTitle: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.receptionTitle,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.receptionSubtitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    receptionSubtitle: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.receptionSubtitle,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.receptionPhone}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, receptionPhone: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="email"
        value={contactMeta.receptionEmail}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, receptionEmail: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>

        {/* Sales */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0057B8] uppercase">{copy.salesCard}</div>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.salesTitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({

                    salesTitle: {

                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },

        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.salesSubtitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    salesSubtitle: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.salesSubtitle,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.salesPhone}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, salesPhone: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <input
        type="email"
        value={contactMeta.salesEmail}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, salesEmail: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="email"
        value={contactMeta.salesOrdersEmail}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, salesOrdersEmail: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>

        {/* Export */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 space-y-2.5">
        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-[#0057B8] uppercase">{copy.exportCard}</div>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.exportTitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    exportTitle: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.exportTitle,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.exportSubtitle[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: {
                    ...prev.contactMeta,
                    exportSubtitle: {
                        ...prev.contactMeta.exportSubtitle,
                        [editLang]: e.target.value,
                    },
                },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.exportPhone}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, exportPhone: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        <input
        type="text"
        value={contactMeta.exportPhoneAlt}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, exportPhoneAlt: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        <input
        type="email"
        value={contactMeta.exportEmail}
        onChange={(e) =>
            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                contactMeta: { ...prev.contactMeta, exportEmail: e.target.value },
            }))
        }
        className="w-full h-8 px-2.5 text-xs font-mono border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
        />
        </div>
        </div>
        </section>

        {/* 2. Directory Entries CRUD */}
        <section className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
        {copy.dirTitle} ({visibleEntries.length})
        </h3>
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
            setEditingEntry(null);
            setCreatingEntry(
                createEmptyEntry(groupFilter === 'all' ? 'departments' : groupFilter)
            );
        }}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494]"
        >
        <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
        <span>{copy.addContact}</span>
        </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {(['all', 'sales', 'management', 'departments', 'branches'] as const).map((g) => (
            <button
            key={g}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setGroupFilter(g)}
            className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg border transition-colors ${
                groupFilter === g
                ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8]'
                : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40'
            }`}
            >
            {copy.groups[g]}
            </button>
        ))}
        </div>

        {creatingEntry && renderEntryForm(creatingEntry, setCreatingEntry, true)}
        {editingEntry && renderEntryForm(editingEntry, setEditingEntry, false)}

        <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto bg-white">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
        <thead>
        <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 font-semibold text-slate-600">
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Подразделение / карточка</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Раздел</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Телефоны и e-mail</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Действия</th>
        </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
        {visibleEntries.map((entry) => (
            <tr key={entry.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70">
            <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
            <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{localize(entry.title)}</div>
            {entry.person && localize(entry.person) && (
                <div className="text-slate-500 mt-0.5">{localize(entry.person)}</div>
            )}
            </td>
            <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-500">{copy.groups[entry.group]}</td>
            <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-700 tabular-nums">
            <div>{entry.phones.map((p) => p.number).join(' · ') || '—'}</div>
            {entry.emails && entry.emails.length > 0 && (
                <div className="text-[#0057B8]">
                {entry.emails.map((em) => em.address).join(' · ')}
                </div>
            )}
            </td>
            <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-right">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
                setCreatingEntry(null);
                setEditingEntry(entry);
            }}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#0057B8]"
            >
            <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => toggleDirectoryEntryVisibility(entry.id)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
            >
            {entry.visible ? (
                <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            ) : (
                <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => deleteDirectoryEntry(entry.id)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
            >
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
            </div>
            </td>
            </tr>
        ))}
        </tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
        </section>

        {/* 3. Interactive Map Locations */}
        <section className="space-y-4">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
        {copy.locationsTitle}
        </h3>

        {editingLocation && (
            <form
            onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                updateLocation(editingLocation);
                setEditingLocation(null);
            }}
            className="border border-[#0057B8]/40 rounded-2xl p-5 bg-blue-50/40 space-y-3"
            >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
            {localize(editingLocation.city)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono text-[#0057B8]">
            {editLang.toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <input
            type="text"
            value={editingLocation.city[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                setEditingLocation({
                    ...editingLocation,
                    city: { ...editingLocation.city, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                })
            }
            placeholder="Город / Площадка"
            className="h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
            type="text"
            value={editingLocation.phone}
            onChange={(e) =>
                setEditingLocation({ ...editingLocation, phone: e.target.value })
            }
            placeholder="+375 ..."
            className="h-9 px-3 text-sm font-mono bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
            type="text"
            value={editingLocation.address[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                setEditingLocation({
                    ...editingLocation,
                    address: { ...editingLocation.address, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                })
            }
            placeholder="Адрес"
            className="h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            <input
            type="text"
            value={editingLocation.hours[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                setEditingLocation({
                    ...editingLocation,
                    hours: { ...editingLocation.hours, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                })
            }
            placeholder="Режим работы"
            className="h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />
            </div>

            <input
            type="text"
            value={editingLocation.focus[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                setEditingLocation({
                    ...editingLocation,
                    focus: { ...editingLocation.focus, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                })
            }
            placeholder="Специализация площадки"
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            />

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditingLocation(null)}
            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg"
            >
            {copy.cancel}
            </button>
            <button
            type="submit"
            className="px-4 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg"
            >
            {copy.save}
            </button>
            </div>
            </form>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
        {locations.map((loc) => (
            <div
            key={loc.id}
            className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
            >
            <div className="space-y-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
            <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
            <span className="truncate">{localize(loc.city)}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-600">{localize(loc.address)}</div>
            <div className="text-xs font-mono text-[#0057B8] tabular-nums">
            {loc.phone} · {localize(loc.hours)}
            </div>
            </div>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditingLocation(loc)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#0057B8] shrink-0"
            >
            <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            </div>
        ))}
        </div>
        </section>
        </div>
    );
};

```
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff, Sparkles, ArrowRight, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useLanguage } from '../../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { Language, PromoBannerTheme } from '../../types';
import { ImagePickerField } from './ImagePickerField';

const LANGS: Language[] = ['ru', 'be', 'en', 'zh'];

export const AdminHomeTab: React.FC = () => {
    const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
    const { siteSettings, updateSiteSettings } = useCatalogStore();
    const [editLang, setEditLang] = useState<Language>(language);

    const { homeHero, promoBanner } = siteSettings;

    const copy = {
        ru: {
            heading: 'Контент главной страницы',
            openHome: 'Открыть главную',
            heroCardTitle: 'Hero-секция (Title, Subtitle и кнопки)',
            heroTitleLabel: 'Главный заголовок H1 (Title)',

    const themePreviewClass: Record<PromoBannerTheme, string> = {
        brand: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0057B8] via-[#0a65c2] to-[#0284c7] text-white',
        slate: 'bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-slate-800 to-slate-700 text-white',
        light: 'bg-blue-50/70 border border-blue-200 text-slate-900',
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{copy.heading}</h2>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <div className="inline-flex bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg p-0.5">
        {LANGS.map((l) => (
            <button
            key={l}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setEditLang(l)}
            className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold rounded-md transition-colors ${
                editLang === l ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
            }`}
            >
            {l.toUpperCase()}
            </button>
        ))}
        </div>

        <Link
        to="/"
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] bg-blue-50 rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-100 transition-colors"
        >
        <span>{copy.openHome}</span>
        <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </Link>
        </div>
        </div>

        {/* 1. Hero Title & Subtitle Editor */}
        <section className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-6 bg-slate-50/70 space-y-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
        {copy.heroCardTitle}
        </h3>
        <span className="text-xs font-mono font-semibold text-[#0057B8]">
        {editLang.toUpperCase()}
        </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.heroTitleLabel} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={homeHero.title[editLang] || ''}

            updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                ...prev,
                homeHero: {
                    ...prev.homeHero,
                    title: { ...prev.homeHero.title, [editLang]: e.target.value },

        }
        className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
        />
        </div>

        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.heroSubtitleLabel} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})

        rows={3}
        value={homeHero.subtitle[editLang] || ''}

                    subtitle: { ...prev.homeHero.subtitle, [editLang]: e.target.value },

                homeHero: {

                    primaryCta: { ...prev.homeHero.primaryCta, [editLang]: e.target.value },

        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.heroSecondaryBtn} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        value={homeHero.secondaryCta[editLang] || ''}
        onChange={(e) =>

                homeHero: {

        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.seoTitle} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"

            themePreviewClass[promoBanner.theme]
        } ${promoBanner.enabled ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-45 grayscale'}`}
        >
        <div className="space-y-1 min-w-0">
        <div
        className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider ${
            promoBanner.theme === 'light' ? 'text-[#0057B8]' : 'text-blue-100'

        </p>

            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg border ${

                : 'bg-white/15 border-white/25 text-white'

            >

            ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white'

        {promoBanner.primaryCtaLabel[editLang] ||

            </div>
            </div>
            </div>

            {/* Fields */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoBadge} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.badge[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        badge: { ...prev.promoBanner.badge, [editLang]: e.target.value },

                }))

            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoExtra} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.extra[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        extra: { ...prev.promoBanner.extra, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                    },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>

            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.themeLabel}
            </label>
            <select
            value={promoBanner.theme}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        theme: e.target.value as PromoBannerTheme,
                    },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            >
            <option value="brand">{copy.themes.brand}</option>
            <option value="slate">{copy.themes.slate}</option>
            <option value="light">{copy.themes.light}</option>
            </select>
            </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoTitle} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.title[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        title: { ...prev.promoBanner.title, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                    },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>

            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoLead} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <textarea
            rows={2}
            value={promoBanner.lead[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        lead: { ...prev.promoBanner.lead, [editLang]: e.target.value },
                    },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full p-2.5 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoSecText} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.secondaryCtaLabel[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        secondaryCtaLabel: {
                            ...prev.promoBanner.secondaryCtaLabel,
                            [editLang]: e.target.value,
                        },
                    },

            />

            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoSecLink}
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.secondaryCtaTo}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: { ...prev.promoBanner, secondaryCtaTo: e.target.value },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm font-mono bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>

            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoPrimText} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.primaryCtaLabel[editLang] || ''}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: {
                        ...prev.promoBanner,
                        primaryCtaLabel: {
                            ...prev.promoBanner.primaryCtaLabel,
                            [editLang]: e.target.value,
                        },
                    },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>

            <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
            {copy.promoPrimLink}
            </label>
            <input
            type="text"
            value={promoBanner.primaryCtaTo}
            onChange={(e) =>
                updateSiteSettings((prev) => ({
                    ...prev,
                    promoBanner: { ...prev.promoBanner, primaryCtaTo: e.target.value },
                }))
            }
            className="w-full h-9 px-3 text-sm font-mono bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
            />
            </div>
            </div>
            </section>
            </div>
    );
};

        </div>

        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.subtitle} ({editLang.toUpperCase()})
        </label>

        />
        </div>
        </div>
        </div>

        {creatingMember && renderMemberForm(creatingMember, setCreatingMember, true)}
        {editingMember && renderMemberForm(editingMember, setEditingMember, false)}

        {/* Team Members Table */}
        <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto bg-white">
        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
        <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600">
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Специалист</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Контакты</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4">Статус</th>
        <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Действия</th>
        </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
        {teamMembers.map((member, idx) => (
            <tr key={member.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70">
            <td className="py-3 px-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {member.photo ? (
                <img
                src={member.photo}
                alt={localize(member.name)}
                className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0"
                />
            ) : (
                <div
                className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center font-display text-xs font-bold shrink-0 ${member.accentClass}`}
                >
                {member.initials}
                </div>
            )}
            <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{localize(member.name)}</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{localize(member.role)}</div>
            </div>
            </div>
            </td>
            <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs font-mono text-slate-600 tabular-nums">
            <div>{member.phone}</div>
            <div className="text-[#0057B8]">{member.email}</div>
            </td>
            <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs">
            <span
            className={
                member.visible ? 'text-[#0057B8] font-medium' : 'text-slate-400'
            }
            >
            {member.visible ? copy.visible : copy.hidden}
            </span>
            </td>
            <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
            <button
            type="button"
            disabled={idx === 0}
            onClick={() => moveTeamMember(member.id, 'up')}
            title="Move up"
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-30"
            >
            <ArrowUp className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            disabled={idx === teamMembers.length - 1}
            onClick={() => moveTeamMember(member.id, 'down')}
            title="Move down"
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-30"
            >
            <ArrowDown className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
                setCreatingMember(null);
                setEditingMember(member);
            }}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-[#0057B8]"
            >
            <Edit3 className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => toggleTeamMemberVisibility(member.id)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100"
            >
            {member.visible ? (
                <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />
            ) : (
                <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" />
            )}
            </button>
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => deleteTeamMember(member.id)}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-md text-slate-500 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
            >
            <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            </div>
            </td>
            </tr>
        ))}
        </tbody>
        </table>
        </div>
        </div>
    );
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/admin/ImagePickerField.tsx`:
```
import React, { useId, useState } from 'react';
import { Image as ImageIcon, Link2, Trash2, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { ASSETS } from '../../data/assets';
import { useLanguage } from '../../hooks/useLanguage';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../ImageWithFallback';

interface ImagePickerFieldProps {
    label: string;
    value: string;
    onChange: (nextValue: string) => void;
    allowClear?: boolean;
    helperText?: string;
}

const PRESET_IMAGES = [
    { key: 'mozzarella', label: 'Моцарелла', src: ASSETS.productMozzarella },
{ key: 'cheese', label: 'Выдержанный сыр', src: ASSETS.productAgedCheese },
{ key: 'milk', label: 'Молоко и кефир', src: ASSETS.productMilkKefir },
{ key: 'butter', label: 'Масло и сливки', src: ASSETS.productButterCream },
{ key: 'yogurt', label: 'Йогурт / творог', src: ASSETS.productGreekYogurt },
{ key: 'ice', label: 'Мороженое', src: ASSETS.productIceCream },
{ key: 'facility', label: 'Производство', src: ASSETS.newsDairyFacility },
{ key: 'hero', label: 'Витрина (Hero)', src: ASSETS.heroDairyShowcase },
] as const;

function compressImageFile(file: File, maxSide = 1200): Promise<string> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('File read error'));
        reader.onload = () => {
            const dataUrl = String(reader.result || '');
            if (!dataUrl || file.type === 'image/svg+xml') {
                resolve(dataUrl);
                return;
            }
            const img = new window.Image();
            img.onload = () => {
                const scale = Math.min(1, maxSide / Math.max(img.width, img.height));
                const width = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.width * scale));
                const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(img.height * scale));
                const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                canvas.width = width;
                canvas.height = height;
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                if (!ctx) {
                    resolve(dataUrl);
                    return;
                }
                ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
                const targetMime = file.type === 'image/png' ? 'image/webp' : 'image/jpeg';
                const compressed = canvas.toDataURL(targetMime, 0.84);
                resolve(compressed.length < dataUrl.length ? compressed : dataUrl);
            };
            img.onerror = () => resolve(dataUrl);
            img.src = dataUrl;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
}

export const ImagePickerField: React.FC<ImagePickerFieldProps> = ({
    label,
    value,
    onChange,
    allowClear = false,
    helperText,
}) => {
    const { language } = useLanguage();
    const inputId = useId();
    const [showPresets, setShowPresets] = useState(false);
    const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

    const copy = {
        ru: {
            upload: 'Загрузить файл',
            loading: 'Сжатие…',
            presets: 'Галерея сайта',
            hidePresets: 'Скрыть галерею',
            urlPlaceholder: 'Или вставьте URL изображения (https://… или /assets/…)',
            clear: 'Убрать фото',
        },
        be: {
            upload: 'Загрузіць файл',
            loading: 'Сціск…',
            presets: 'Галерэя сайта',
            hidePresets: 'Схаваць галерэю',
            urlPlaceholder: 'Або ўстаўце URL выявы (https://…)',
            clear: 'Прыбраць фота',
        },
        en: {
            upload: 'Upload file',
            loading: 'Processing…',
            presets: 'Site library',
            hidePresets: 'Hide library',
            urlPlaceholder: 'Or paste image URL (https://…)',
            clear: 'Remove',
        },
        zh: {
            upload: '上传图片',
            loading: '处理中…',
            presets: '媒体库预设',
            hidePresets: '收起媒体库',
            urlPlaceholder: '或粘贴图片链接 (https://…)',
            clear: '移除图片',
        },
    }[language];

    const handleFileChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        setBusy(true);
        try {
            const optimized = await compressImageFile(file);
            if (optimized) onChange(optimized);
        } finally {
            setBusy(false);
            e.target.value = '';
        }
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <label htmlFor={inputId} className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
        {label}
        </label>
        {helperText && <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{helperText}</span>}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
        <div className="w-24 h-18 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 shrink-0">
        <ImageWithFallback
        src={value}
        alt={label}
        className="w-full h-full object-cover"
        />
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 w-full space-y-2">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <label className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition-colors">
        <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>{busy ? copy.loading : copy.upload}</span>
        <input
        type="file"
        accept="image/*"
        onChange={handleFileChange}
        className="hidden"
        />
        </label>

        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setShowPresets((prev) => !prev)}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 rounded-lg transition-colors"
        >
        <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>{showPresets ? copy.hidePresets : copy.presets}</span>
        </button>

        {allowClear && value && (
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange('')}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>{copy.clear}</span>
            </button>
        )}
        </div>

        <div className="relative">
        <Link2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
        <input
        id={inputId}
        type="text"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={copy.urlPlaceholder}
        className="w-full h-8 pl-8 pr-2.5 text-xs bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:bg-white focus:border-[#0057B8]"
        />
        </div>
        </div>
        </div>

        {showPresets && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-4 sm:grid-cols-8 gap-2 p-2.5 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl">
            {PRESET_IMAGES.map((preset) => {
                const selected = value === preset.src;
                return (
                    <button
                    key={preset.key}
                    type="button"
                    title={preset.label}
                    onClick={() => {
                        onChange(preset.src);
                        setShowPresets(false);
                    }}
                    className={`group relative aspect-[4/3] rounded-lg overflow-hidden border transition-all ${
                        selected
                        ? 'border-[#0057B8] ring-2 ring-[#0057B8]/30'
                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/50'
                    }`}
                    >
                    <img
                    src={preset.src}
                    alt={preset.label}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform"
                    />
                    <span className="sr-only">{preset.label}</span>
                    </button>
                );
            })}
            </div>
        )}
        </div>
    );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { Product } from '../types';

interface B2BInquiryModalProps {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  product?: Product;
  selectedPackageId?: string;
  defaultChannel?: 'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general';
}

export const B2BInquiryModal: React.FC<B2BInquiryModalProps> = ({
  open,
  onClose,
  product,
  selectedPackageId,
  defaultChannel = 'wholesale',
}) => {
  const { language, localize } = useLanguage();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(open);
  const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false);

  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');

          titleExport: 'Запыт у аддзел экспарту',

      setClosing(true);

        setClosing(false);
      }, 180);

    }
  }, [open, mounted]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    setSubmitted(false);
    setError('');
    setCountry((prev) => prev || copy.defaultCountry);
    setVolume((prev) => prev || copy.defaultVolume);

    if (product) {
      const pkg = product.packages.find((p) => p.id === selectedPackageId) || product.packages[0];
      setMessage(`${copy.prefix} ${localize(product.name)} (${pkg?.weight || ''})`);
    } else {
      setMessage('');
    }
  }, [open, product, selectedPackageId, language, localize, copy.defaultCountry, copy.defaultVolume, copy.prefix]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
      window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
      return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [open, onClose]);

  if (!mounted) return null;

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!company.trim() || !contactName.trim() || !email.includes('@')) {
      setError(copy.err);
      return;
    }
    setError('');
    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div
    className={`fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-slate-900/55 backdrop-blur-xs ${
      closing ? 'modal-backdrop-out pointer-events-none' : 'modal-backdrop-in'
    }`}
    role="dialog"
    aria-modal="true"
    onClick={(e) => {
      if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
    }}
    >
    <div
    className={`bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto shadow-2xl ${
      closing ? 'modal-panel-out' : 'modal-panel-in'
    }`}
    >
    <div className="px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
    <div>
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    {defaultChannel === 'export' ? copy.titleExport : copy.titleSales}
    </h2>
    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">ved@milk.by · sales@milk.by</p>
    </div>
    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={onClose}
    aria-label="Close modal"
    className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors"
    >
    <X className="w-4 h-4" />
    </button>
    </div>

    <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
    {submitted ? (
      <div className="py-6 text-center space-y-4">
      <CheckCircle2 className="w-12 h-12 text-[#0057B8] mx-auto" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      <div className="space-y-1">
      <h3 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
      {copy.sentTitle}
      </h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.sentDesc(company, email)}</p>
      </div>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClose}
      className="px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
      >
      {copy.close}
      </button>

    ) : (
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      {error && (
        <div className="p-3 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg">

      {copy.company}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      required
      value={company}
      onChange={(e) => setCompany(e.target.value)}
      placeholder={copy.companyPlaceholder}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      <div>

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/CategoryIcon.tsx`:

import React from 'react';

interface CategoryIconProps {
    categoryId: string;
    className?: string;
}

export const CategoryIcon: React.FC<CategoryIconProps> = ({
    categoryId,
    className = 'w-9 h-9',
}) => {
    switch (categoryId) {
        case 'all':
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="8" y="8" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                <rect
                x="26.5"
                y="8"
                width="13.5"
                height="13.5"
                rx="4"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.4"
                />
                <rect x="8" y="26.5" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                <rect x="26.5" y="26.5" width="13.5" height="13.5" rx="4" fill="currentColor" />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'milk':
            // Bottle silhouette with curved milk wave (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <rect x="19" y="6" width="10" height="3.5" rx="1.75" fill="currentColor" />
                <path
                d="M20 9.5H28V13.2C28 15.6 33 17.6 33 22.8V24.6C29.5 22.8 26.8 22.8 24 24.6C21.2 26.4 18.5 26.4 15 24.6V22.8C15 17.6 20 15.6 20 13.2V9.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.28"
                />
                <path
                d="M15 24.6C18.5 26.4 21.2 26.4 24 24.6C26.8 22.8 29.5 22.8 33 24.6V37.5C33 39.985 30.985 42 28.5 42H19.5C17.015 42 15 39.985 15 37.5V24.6Z"

                fillOpacity="0.25"
                />
                {/* Separated front wedge */}
                <path
                d="M26.5 24.2L41 23.6C40.4 26.9 36.3 29.7 31 30.9L26.5 24.2Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.58"
                />
                <path
                d="M31 30.9C36.3 29.7 40.4 26.9 41 23.6V31.8C40.4 35.1 36.3 37.9 31 39.1V30.9Z"

                />

                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.8"
                />
                </svg>
            );

        case 'butter':
            // Isometric 3D butter brick on base plate (inspired by milk.by)
            return (
                <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" fill="none" className={className} aria-hidden="true">
                <path
                d="M7 26.8L22 35.2L41 24.8V28.5L22 39L7 30.5V26.8Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.35"
                />
                <path
                d="M11 21.5L24.2 14L37.4 21.5L24.2 29L11 21.5Z"
                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.55"
                />
                <path d="M11 21.5L24.2 29V34.8L11 27.3V21.5Z" fill="currentColor" />

                fill="currentColor"
                fillOpacity="0.88"
                />
                </svg>
            );
    }
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/components/Filters.tsx`:

import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { RotateCcw, SlidersHorizontal, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
import { Category, Product } from '../types';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';

export interface CatalogFilterState {
  categoryId: string;
  subcategoryId: string;
  fatRange: 'all' | 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';
  weightRange: 'all' | 'retail' | 'medium' | 'bulk';
  shelfLifeRange: 'all' | 'fresh' | 'medium' | 'long';
  proteinMin: 'all' | '8' | '15' | '24';
  lactoseFreeOnly: boolean;
  highProteinOnly: boolean;
  exportReadyOnly: boolean;
  characteristics: Record<string, string>;
  sortBy: 'featured' | 'fat-asc' | 'fat-desc' | 'protein-desc' | 'name';
}

interface FiltersProps {
  categories: Category[];
  products: Product[];
  state: CatalogFilterState;
  onChange: (next: CatalogFilterState) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
  totalCount: number;
}

export const Filters: React.FC<FiltersProps> = ({
  categories,
  products,
  state,
  onChange,
  onReset,
  totalCount,
}) => {
