import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Eye, EyeOff, Lock, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS, useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { SeoHead } from './SeoHead';

interface StaffLoginFormProps {
    onSuccess?: () => void;
    onCancel?: () => void;
}

const StaffLoginForm: React.FC<StaffLoginFormProps> = ({ onSuccess, onCancel }) => {
    const { language } = useLanguage();
    const { login } = useAuth();

    const [identifier, setIdentifier] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState('');

    const copy = {
        ru: {
            loginLabel: 'Логин или e-mail',
            loginPlaceholder: 'admin',
            passwordLabel: 'Пароль',
            passwordPlaceholder: 'Введите пароль',
            showPwd: 'Показать пароль',

        },
        be: {

            showPwd: 'Паказаць пароль',
            hidePwd: 'Схаваць пароль',
            errInvalid: 'Няправільны лагін або пароль.',
            submit: 'Увайсці',
            cancel: 'Адмена',
            demoHint: 'Дэма-доступ:',
            fillDemo: 'Запоўніць',
        },
        en: {
            loginLabel: 'Username or email',
            loginPlaceholder: 'admin',
            passwordLabel: 'Password',
            passwordPlaceholder: 'Enter password',
            showPwd: 'Show password',
            hidePwd: 'Hide password',
            errInvalid: 'Invalid username or password.',
            submit: 'Sign In',
            cancel: 'Cancel',
            demoHint: 'Demo access:',
            fillDemo: 'Autofill',
        },
        zh: {
            loginLabel: '用户名或邮箱',
            loginPlaceholder: 'admin',
            passwordLabel: '密码',
            passwordPlaceholder: '请输入密码',
            showPwd: '显示密码',
            hidePwd: '隐藏密码',
            errInvalid: '用户名或密码错误。',
            submit: '登录',
            cancel: '取消',
            demoHint: '演示账号:',
            fillDemo: '自动填充',
        },
    }[language];

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const ok = login(identifier, password);
        if (!ok) {
            setError(copy.errInvalid);
            return;
        }
        setError('');
        onSuccess?.();
    };

    return (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
        {error && (
            <div className="p-3 text-xs font-medium text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg">
            {error}
            </div>
        )}

        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.loginLabel}
        </label>
        <input
        type="text"
        required
        autoComplete="username"
        value={identifier}
        onChange={(e) => {
            setIdentifier(e.target.value);
            if (error) setError('');
        }}
        placeholder={copy.loginPlaceholder}
        className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
        />
        </div>

        <div>
        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
        {copy.passwordLabel}
        </label>
        <div className="relative">
        <input
        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
        required
        autoComplete="current-password"
        value={password}
        onChange={(e) => {
            setPassword(e.target.value);
            if (error) setError('');
        }}
        placeholder={copy.passwordPlaceholder}
        className="w-full h-10 pl-3 pr-10 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
        />
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setShowPassword((prev) => !prev)}
        aria-label={showPassword ? copy.hidePwd : copy.showPwd}
        className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
        >
        {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
        </button>
        </div>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-1 flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5">
        {onCancel && (
            <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
            >
            {copy.cancel}
            </button>
        )}
        <button
        type="submit"
        className={`${
            onCancel ? 'px-5' : 'w-full'
        } py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors`}
        >
        {copy.submit}
        </button>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
        <span className="font-mono">
        {copy.demoHint} {DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.username} / {DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.password}
        </span>
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
            setIdentifier(DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.username);
            setPassword(DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.password);
            setError('');
        }}
        className="font-medium text-[#0057B8]/85 hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
        >
        {copy.fillDemo}
        </button>
        </div>
        </form>
    );
};

interface StaffAuthModalProps {
    open: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSuccess?: () => void;
}

export const StaffAuthModal: React.FC<StaffAuthModalProps> = ({
    open,
    onClose,
    onSuccess,
}) => {
    const { language } = useLanguage();
    const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(open);
    const [closing, setClosing] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (open) {
            setMounted(true);
            setClosing(false);
            return;
        }

        if (mounted) {
            setClosing(true);
            const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
                setMounted(false);
                setClosing(false);
            }, 180);
            return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
        }
    }, [open, mounted]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open) return;
        const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
        };
            window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
            return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    }, [open, onClose]);

    if (!mounted) return null;

    const copy = {
        ru: {
            title: 'Вход для сотрудников',
            subtitle: 'Панель управления каталогом и материалами сайта',
        },
        be: {
            title: 'Уваход для супрацоўнікаў',
            subtitle: 'Панэль кіравання каталогам і матэрыяламі сайта',
        },
        en: {
            title: 'Staff Sign In',
            subtitle: 'Access catalog and website content management',
        },
        zh: {
            title: '员工登录',
            subtitle: '进入产品目录与网站内容管理后台',
        },
    }[language];

    return (
        <div
        className={`fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-slate-900/55 backdrop-blur-xs ${
            closing ? 'modal-backdrop-out pointer-events-none' : 'modal-backdrop-in'
        }`}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        onClick={(e) => {
            if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose();
        }}
        >
        <div
        className={`bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl overflow-hidden ${
            closing ? 'modal-panel-out' : 'modal-panel-in'
        }`}
        >
        <div className="px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
        <Lock className="w-4 h-4" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
        <h2 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 truncate">
        {copy.title}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 truncate">{copy.subtitle}</p>
        </div>
        </div>

        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onClose}
        aria-label="Close modal"
        className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 transition-colors shrink-0"
        >
        <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">
        <StaffLoginForm
        onCancel={onClose}
        onSuccess={() => {
            onClose();
            onSuccess?.();
        }}
        />
        </div>
        </div>
        </div>
    );
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/hooks/useAuth.tsx`:
```
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useState } from 'react';
import { Language } from '../types';

export const DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS = {
    username: 'admin',
    emailAlias: 'admin@milk.by',
    password: 'milk2026',
} as const;

export interface StaffUser {
    username: string;
    role: Record<Language, string>;
}

interface AuthContextValue {
    user: StaffUser | null;
    isAuthenticated: boolean;
    login: (identifier: string, password: string) => boolean;
    logout: () => void;
}

const STORAGE_AUTH_KEY = 'milk_world_staff_auth_v1';

const DEFAULT_STAFF_USER: StaffUser = {
    username: DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.username,
    role: {
        ru: 'Администратор сайта',
        be: 'Адміністратар сайта',
        en: 'Site Administrator',
        zh: '网站管理员',
    },
};

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

function loadAuthSession(): StaffUser | null {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_AUTH_KEY);
        if (!raw) return null;
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { username?: string };
        if (parsed?.username === DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.username) {
            return DEFAULT_STAFF_USER;
        }
    } catch {
        // ignore storage errors
    }
    return null;
}

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
    const [user, setUser] = useState<StaffUser | null>(() => loadAuthSession());

    const login = useCallback((identifier: string, password: string): boolean => {
        const normalized = identifier.trim().toLowerCase();
        const isValidUser =
        normalized === DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.username ||
        normalized === DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.emailAlias;
        const isValidPassword = password === DEMO_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS.password;

        if (!isValidUser || !isValidPassword) {
            return false;
        }

        setUser(DEFAULT_STAFF_USER);
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(
                STORAGE_AUTH_KEY,
                JSON.stringify({ username: DEFAULT_STAFF_USER.username })
            );
        } catch {
            // ignore storage errors
        }
        return true;
    }, []);

    const logout = useCallback(() => {
        setUser(null);
        try {
            localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_AUTH_KEY);
        } catch {
            // ignore storage errors
        }
    }, []);

    return (
        <AuthContext.Provider
        value={{
            user,
            isAuthenticated: Boolean(user),
            login,
            logout,
        }}
        >
        {children}
        </AuthContext.Provider>
    );
};

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
    const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
    if (!ctx) {
        throw new Error('useAuth must be used within an AuthProvider');
    }
    return ctx;
}
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/hooks/useCatalogStore.tsx`:
```
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Category,
  DirectoryEntry,
  NewsArticle,
  PlantLocation,
  Product,
  RedirectRule,
  SiteSettings,
  TeamMember,
} from '../types';
import { products as initialProducts, legacyRedirects as initialRedirects } from '../data/products';
import { categories as initialCategories } from '../data/categories';
import { newsArticles as initialNews } from '../data/news';
import {
  defaultDirectoryEntries,
  defaultLocations,
  defaultSiteSettings,
  defaultTeamMembers,
} from '../data/siteContent';

interface CatalogStoreContextValue {
  products: Product[];
  categories: Category[];
  news: NewsArticle[];
  redirects: RedirectRule[];
  siteSettings: SiteSettings;
  teamMembers: TeamMember[];
  directoryEntries: DirectoryEntry[];
  locations: PlantLocation[];
  addProduct: (product: Product) => void;
  updateProduct: (product: Product) => void;
  importProducts: (items: Product[]) => void;
  toggleProductVisibility: (id: string) => void;
  deleteProduct: (id: string) => void;
  addCategory: (category: Category) => void;
  updateCategory: (category: Category) => void;
  deleteCategory: (id: string) => void;
  addNews: (article: NewsArticle) => void;
  updateNews: (article: NewsArticle) => void;
  toggleNewsPublished: (id: string) => void;
  deleteNews: (id: string) => void;
  addRedirect: (rule: RedirectRule) => void;
  updateRedirect: (index: number, rule: RedirectRule) => void;
  deleteRedirect: (index: number) => void;
  updateSiteSettings: (
    next: SiteSettings | ((prev: SiteSettings) => SiteSettings)
  ) => void;
  addTeamMember: (member: TeamMember) => void;
  updateTeamMember: (member: TeamMember) => void;
  toggleTeamMemberVisibility: (id: string) => void;
  moveTeamMember: (id: string, direction: 'up' | 'down') => void;
  deleteTeamMember: (id: string) => void;
  addDirectoryEntry: (entry: DirectoryEntry) => void;
  updateDirectoryEntry: (entry: DirectoryEntry) => void;
  toggleDirectoryEntryVisibility: (id: string) => void;
  deleteDirectoryEntry: (id: string) => void;
  addLocation: (location: PlantLocation) => void;
  updateLocation: (location: PlantLocation) => void;
  deleteLocation: (id: string) => void;
  resetDemoData: () => void;
}

const STORAGE_PRODUCTS_KEY = 'milk_world_products_v2';
const STORAGE_CATEGORIES_KEY = 'milk_world_categories_v2';
const STORAGE_NEWS_KEY = 'milk_world_news_v2';
const STORAGE_REDIRECTS_KEY = 'milk_world_redirects_v2';
const STORAGE_SITE_SETTINGS_KEY = 'milk_world_site_settings_v2';
const STORAGE_TEAM_KEY = 'milk_world_team_v2';
const STORAGE_CONTACTS_KEY = 'milk_world_contacts_v2';
const STORAGE_LOCATIONS_KEY = 'milk_world_locations_v2';

const CatalogStoreContext = createContext<CatalogStoreContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

function loadStorage<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
    if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    // ignore storage errors
  }
  return fallback;
}

function loadSiteSettings(): SiteSettings {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_SITE_SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<SiteSettings>;
      return {
        homeHero: { ...defaultSiteSettings.homeHero, ...(parsed.homeHero || {}) },
        promoBanner: { ...defaultSiteSettings.promoBanner, ...(parsed.promoBanner || {}) },
        teamSection: { ...defaultSiteSettings.teamSection, ...(parsed.teamSection || {}) },
        contactMeta: { ...defaultSiteSettings.contactMeta, ...(parsed.contactMeta || {}) },
        footer: { ...defaultSiteSettings.footer, ...(parsed.footer || {}) },
      };
    }
  } catch {
    // ignore storage errors
  }
  return defaultSiteSettings;
}

function saveStorage<T>(key: string, value: T): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    // ignore storage errors
  }
}

export const CatalogStoreProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_PRODUCTS_KEY, initialProducts)
  );
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_CATEGORIES_KEY, initialCategories)
  );
  const [news, setNews] = useState<NewsArticle[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_NEWS_KEY, initialNews)
  );
  const [redirects, setRedirects] = useState<RedirectRule[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_REDIRECTS_KEY, initialRedirects)
  );
  const [siteSettings, setSiteSettings] = useState<SiteSettings>(loadSiteSettings);
  const [teamMembers, setTeamMembers] = useState<TeamMember[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_TEAM_KEY, defaultTeamMembers)
  );
  const [directoryEntries, setDirectoryEntries] = useState<DirectoryEntry[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_CONTACTS_KEY, defaultDirectoryEntries)
  );
  const [locations, setLocations] = useState<PlantLocation[]>(() =>
  loadStorage(STORAGE_LOCATIONS_KEY, defaultLocations)
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_PRODUCTS_KEY, products);
  }, [products]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_CATEGORIES_KEY, categories);
  }, [categories]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_NEWS_KEY, news);
  }, [news]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_REDIRECTS_KEY, redirects);
  }, [redirects]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_SITE_SETTINGS_KEY, siteSettings);
  }, [siteSettings]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_TEAM_KEY, teamMembers);
  }, [teamMembers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_CONTACTS_KEY, directoryEntries);
  }, [directoryEntries]);

  useEffect(() => {
    saveStorage(STORAGE_LOCATIONS_KEY, locations);
  }, [locations]);

  const addProduct = useCallback((product: Product) => {
    setProducts((prev) => [product, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateProduct = useCallback((updated: Product) => {
    setProducts((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === updated.id ? updated : p)));
  }, []);

  const importProducts = useCallback((items: Product[]) => {
    setProducts((prev) => {
      const map = new Map(prev.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
      for (const item of items) {
        map.set(item.id, item);
      }
      return Array.from(map.values());
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleProductVisibility = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setProducts((prev) =>
    prev.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, visible: !p.visible } : p))
    );
  }, []);

  const deleteProduct = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setProducts((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addCategory = useCallback((category: Category) => {
    setCategories((prev) => [...prev, category]);
  }, []);

  const updateCategory = useCallback((updated: Category) => {
    setCategories((prev) => prev.map((c) => (c.id === updated.id ? updated : c)));
  }, []);

  const deleteCategory = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setCategories((prev) => prev.filter((c) => c.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addNews = useCallback((article: NewsArticle) => {
    setNews((prev) => [article, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateNews = useCallback((updated: NewsArticle) => {
    setNews((prev) => prev.map((n) => (n.id === updated.id ? updated : n)));
  }, []);

  const toggleNewsPublished = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setNews((prev) =>
    prev.map((n) => (n.id === id ? { ...n, published: !n.published } : n))
    );
  }, []);

  const deleteNews = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setNews((prev) => prev.filter((n) => n.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addRedirect = useCallback((rule: RedirectRule) => {
    setRedirects((prev) => [rule, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateRedirect = useCallback((index: number, rule: RedirectRule) => {
    setRedirects((prev) => prev.map((r, i) => (i === index ? rule : r)));
  }, []);

  const deleteRedirect = useCallback((index: number) => {
    setRedirects((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  }, []);

  const updateSiteSettings = useCallback(
    (next: SiteSettings | ((prev: SiteSettings) => SiteSettings)) => {
      setSiteSettings((prev) => (typeof next === 'function' ? next(prev) : next));
    },
    []
  );

  const addTeamMember = useCallback((member: TeamMember) => {
    setTeamMembers((prev) => [member, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateTeamMember = useCallback((updated: TeamMember) => {
    setTeamMembers((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === updated.id ? updated : m)));
  }, []);

  const toggleTeamMemberVisibility = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setTeamMembers((prev) =>
    prev.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, visible: !m.visible } : m))
    );
  }, []);

  const moveTeamMember = useCallback((id: string, direction: 'up' | 'down') => {
    setTeamMembers((prev) => {
      const idx = prev.findIndex((m) => m.id === id);
      if (idx === -1) return prev;
      const targetIdx = direction === 'up' ? idx - 1 : idx + 1;
      if (targetIdx < 0 || targetIdx >= prev.length) return prev;
      const copy = [...prev];
      const [item] = copy.splice(idx, 1);
      copy.splice(targetIdx, 0, item);
      return copy;
    });
  }, []);

  const deleteTeamMember = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setTeamMembers((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addDirectoryEntry = useCallback((entry: DirectoryEntry) => {
    setDirectoryEntries((prev) => [entry, ...prev]);
  }, []);

  const updateDirectoryEntry = useCallback((updated: DirectoryEntry) => {
    setDirectoryEntries((prev) => prev.map((e) => (e.id === updated.id ? updated : e)));
  }, []);

  const toggleDirectoryEntryVisibility = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setDirectoryEntries((prev) =>
    prev.map((e) => (e.id === id ? { ...e, visible: !e.visible } : e))
    );
  }, []);

  const deleteDirectoryEntry = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setDirectoryEntries((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const addLocation = useCallback((location: PlantLocation) => {
    setLocations((prev) => [...prev, location]);
  }, []);

  const updateLocation = useCallback((updated: PlantLocation) => {
    setLocations((prev) => prev.map((l) => (l.id === updated.id ? updated : l)));
  }, []);

  const deleteLocation = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const resetDemoData = useCallback(() => {
    setProducts(initialProducts);
    setCategories(initialCategories);
    setNews(initialNews);
    setRedirects(initialRedirects);
    setSiteSettings(defaultSiteSettings);
    setTeamMembers(defaultTeamMembers);
    setDirectoryEntries(defaultDirectoryEntries);
    setLocations(defaultLocations);
  }, []);

  return (
    <CatalogStoreContext.Provider
    value={{
      products,
      categories,
      news,
      redirects,
      siteSettings,
      teamMembers,
      directoryEntries,
      locations,
      addProduct,
      updateProduct,
      importProducts,
      toggleProductVisibility,
      deleteProduct,
      addCategory,
      updateCategory,
      deleteCategory,
      addNews,
      updateNews,
      toggleNewsPublished,
      deleteNews,
      addRedirect,
      updateRedirect,
      deleteRedirect,
      updateSiteSettings,
      addTeamMember,
      updateTeamMember,
      toggleTeamMemberVisibility,
      moveTeamMember,
      deleteTeamMember,
      addDirectoryEntry,
      updateDirectoryEntry,
      toggleDirectoryEntryVisibility,
      deleteDirectoryEntry,
      addLocation,
      updateLocation,
      deleteLocation,
      resetDemoData,
    }}
    >
    {children}
    </CatalogStoreContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useCatalogStore(): CatalogStoreContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(CatalogStoreContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error('useCatalogStore must be used within a CatalogStoreProvider');
  }
  return ctx;
}
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/hooks/useLanguage.tsx`:
```
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { Language, LocalizedText } from '../types';
import { defaultTranslations } from '../data/translations';

interface LanguageContextValue {
  language: Language;
  setLanguage: (lang: Language) => void;
  t: (key: string) => string;
  localize: (text?: Partial<LocalizedText> | null) => string;
  translations: Record<string, LocalizedText>;
  updateTranslation: (key: string, lang: Language, value: string) => void;
  resetTranslations: () => void;
}

const STORAGE_LANG_KEY = 'milk_world_lang';
const STORAGE_TRANS_KEY = 'milk_world_translations_v2';

const LanguageContext = createContext<LanguageContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export const LanguageProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [language, setLanguageState] = useState<Language>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_LANG_KEY) as Language | null;
      if (saved && ['ru', 'be', 'en', 'zh'].includes(saved)) {
        return saved;
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore storage errors
    }
    return 'ru';
  });

  const [translations, setTranslations] = useState<Record<string, LocalizedText>>(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_TRANS_KEY);
      if (saved) {
        return { ...defaultTranslations, ...JSON.parse(saved) };
      }
    } catch {
      // ignore storage errors
    }
    return defaultTranslations;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = language;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_LANG_KEY, language);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, [language]);

  const setLanguage = useCallback((lang: Language) => {
    setLanguageState(lang);
  }, []);

  const t = useCallback(
    (key: string): string => {
      const entry = translations[key];
      if (!entry) return key;
      return entry[language] || entry.ru || entry.en || key;
    },
    [language, translations]
  );

  const localize = useCallback(
    (text?: Partial<LocalizedText> | null): string => {
      if (!text) return '';
      return text[language] || text.ru || text.en || text.be || text.zh || '';
    },
    [language]
  );

  const updateTranslation = useCallback((key: string, lang: Language, value: string) => {
    setTranslations((prev) => {
      const updated = {
        ...prev,
        [key]: {
          ...(prev[key] || { ru: '', be: '', en: '', zh: '' }),
                    [lang]: value,
        },
      };
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_TRANS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      } catch {
        // ignore
      }
      return updated;
    });
  }, []);

  const resetTranslations = useCallback(() => {
    setTranslations(defaultTranslations);
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_TRANS_KEY);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider
    value={{
      language,
      setLanguage,
      t,
      localize,
      translations,
      updateTranslation,
      resetTranslations,
    }}
    >
    {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  );
};

export function useLanguage(): LanguageContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(LanguageContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error('useLanguage must be used within a LanguageProvider');
  }
  return ctx;
}
```

const parseWeightGrams = (label: string): number => {
  const num = parseFloat(label.replace(',', '.'));
  if (!Number.isFinite(num) || num <= 0) return 250;
  if (/кг|kg|л|l/i.test(label) && !/мг|мл|ml/i.test(label)) {
    return Math.round(num * 1000);
  }
  return Math.round(num);
};
