    },
  ];

  const tabMeta: { id: DirectoryGroup; icon: React.FC<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
    { id: 'sales', icon: Truck },
    { id: 'management', icon: Users },
    { id: 'departments', icon: Briefcase },
    { id: 'branches', icon: Building2 },
  ];

  const displayedEntries = useMemo(() => {
    const visible = directoryEntries.filter((e) => e.visible);
    const q = directoryQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) {
      return visible.filter((item) => item.group === activeTab);
    }

    return visible.filter((item) => {
      const searchable = [
        item.title.ru,
        item.title.be,
        item.title.en,
        item.title.zh,
        item.person?.ru,
        item.person?.be,
        item.person?.en,
        item.address?.ru,
        item.address?.en,
        item.note?.ru,
        ...item.phones.map((p) => `${p.label?.[language] || ''} ${p.number}`),
                          ...(item.emails?.map((e) => e.address) || []),
                          item.website,
      ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' ')
      .toLowerCase();

      return searchable.includes(q);
    });
  }, [directoryEntries, activeTab, directoryQuery, language]);

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !email.includes('@') || !message.trim() || !consent) return;
    setSent(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-12 space-y-12">
    <SeoHead
    title={`${t('navContacts')} — ${t('brandName')} (milk.by)`}
    description={copy.seoDesc}
    canonicalHash="/contacts"
    />

    {/* 1. Header & Working Hours */}
    <div className="space-y-3 border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.h1}</h1>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
    <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
    <span>
    {localize(contactMeta.hqAddress)} · {copy.unpPrefix} {contactMeta.unp}
    </span>
    </span>
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 tabular-nums">
    <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
    <span>{localize(contactMeta.schedule)}</span>
    </span>
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* 2. Top 3 Primary Channels */}
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
    {topCards.map((card, idx) => (
      <div
      key={`${card.email}-${idx}`}
      className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 bg-slate-50/70 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-4"
      >
      <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0057B8]">
      {idx === 2 ? <Globe2 className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Phone className="w-4 h-4" />}
      <span>milk.by</span>
      </div>
      <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">{card.title}</h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{card.subtitle}</p>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5 font-mono text-sm tabular-nums text-slate-700 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200/80">
      {card.phones.map((phone) => (
        <div key={phone} className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
        <a
        href={`tel:${phone.replace(/[^+\d]/g, '')}`}
        className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors"
        >
        {phone}
        </a>
        </div>
      ))}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
      <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
      <a
      href={`mailto:${card.email}`}
      className="text-[#0057B8] font-semibold hover:underline"
      >
      {card.email}
      </a>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>
    ))}
    </section>

    {/* 3. Complete Interactive Contact Directory */}
    <section className="space-y-6">
    <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-end justify-between gap-4">
    <div className="space-y-1">
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.dirTitle}</h2>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.dirSubtitle}</p>
    </div>

    <div className="relative w-full lg:w-96">
    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
    <input
    type="text"
    value={directoryQuery}
    onChange={(e) => setDirectoryQuery(e.target.value)}
    placeholder={copy.searchPlaceholder}
    className="w-full h-10 pl-10 pr-9 text-base sm:text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0057B8]/15"
    />
    {directoryQuery && (
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setDirectoryQuery('')}
      aria-label="Clear search"
      className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100"
      >
      <X className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    )}
    </div>
    </div>

    <div className="flex overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar gap-2 pb-1">
    {tabMeta.map(({ id, icon: Icon }) => {
      const active = activeTab === id && !directoryQuery.trim();
      return (
        <button
        key={id}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          setDirectoryQuery('');
          setActiveTab(id);
        }}
        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl border text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 transition-colors ${
          active
          ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
          : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
        }`}
        >
        <Icon className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
        <span>{copy.tabs[id]}</span>
        </button>
      );
    })}
    </div>

    {directoryQuery.trim() && (
      <div className="text-xs font-medium text-[#0057B8] bg-blue-50/70 border border-blue-200/70 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 tabular-nums">
      {copy.searchActiveHint(displayedEntries.length)}
      </div>
    )}

    {displayedEntries.length === 0 ? (
      <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/70 p-8 text-center space-y-3">
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.emptyDir}</p>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setDirectoryQuery('')}
      className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg"
      >
      {copy.resetSearch}
      </button>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
      {displayedEntries.map((entry) => (
        <article
        key={entry.id}
        className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 bg-white flex flex-col justify-between gap-3.5 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 transition-colors"
        >
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <h3 className="font-display text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-slate-900 leading-snug">
        {localize(entry.title)}
        </h3>
        {entry.person && localize(entry.person) && (
          <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">{localize(entry.person)}</p>
        )}
        {entry.address && localize(entry.address) && (
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex items-start gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
          <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-slate-400" />
          <span>{localize(entry.address)}</span>
          </p>
        )}
        {entry.note && localize(entry.note) && (
          <p className="text-xs text-[#0057B8] font-medium pt-0.5">
          {localize(entry.note)}
          </p>
        )}
        </div>

        <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-1.5 text-xs font-mono tabular-nums text-slate-700">
        {entry.phones.map((phone, i) => (
          <div key={`${entry.id}-ph-${i}`} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
          {phone.label && localize(phone.label) && (
            <span className="font-sans text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 bg-slate-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
            {localize(phone.label)}
            </span>
          )}
          <a
          href={`tel:${phone.number.replace(/[^+\d]/g, '')}`}
          className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors"
          >
          {phone.number}
          </a>
          </div>
        ))}

        {entry.emails?.map((em) => (
          <div key={em.address} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Mail className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
          {em.label && (
            <span className="font-sans text-[11px] text-slate-500">{em.label}:</span>
          )}
          <a
          href={`mailto:${em.address}`}
          className="text-[#0057B8] hover:underline truncate"
          >
          {em.address}
          </a>
          </div>
        ))}

        {entry.website && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
          <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
          <a
          href={entry.website}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
          className="text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8] hover:underline truncate"
          >
          {entry.website.replace(/^https?:\/\//, '')}
          </a>
          </div>
        )}
        </div>
        </article>
      ))}
      </div>
    )}
    </section>

    {/* 4. HQ Requisites + Direct Feedback Form */}
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 pt-2">
    <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-4">
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 bg-slate-50/70 space-y-3">
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
    <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
    <span>{copy.hqTitle}</span>
    </div>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">
    {t('brandName')} · {copy.unpPrefix} {contactMeta.unp} · {localize(contactMeta.hqAddress)}
    </p>
    <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-200 text-xs text-slate-600 space-y-1.5">
    <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
    <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
    <span>{localize(contactMeta.schedule)}</span>
    </div>
    <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
    <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
    <span>{localize(contactMeta.citizenHours)}</span>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>

    <div className="lg:col-span-7 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 sm:p-8 bg-white">
    {sent ? (
      <div className="py-10 text-center space-y-3">
      <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-[#0057B8] mx-auto" />
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
      {copy.sentTitle}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">{copy.sentDesc}</p>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => {
        setSent(false);
        setMessage('');
      }}
      className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] bg-blue-50 rounded-lg"
      >
      {copy.sendAnother}
      </button>
      </div>
    ) : (
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
      {copy.formTitle}
      </h2>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.nameLabel}
      </label>
      <input
      type="text"
      required
      value={name}
      onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">E-mail *</label>
      <input
      type="email"
      required
      value={email}
      onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>
      </div>

      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.deptLabel}
      </label>
      <select
      value={department}
      onChange={(e) => setDepartment(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg bg-white focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      >
      <option value="sales">{copy.deptOptions.sales}</option>
      <option value="export">{copy.deptOptions.export}</option>
      <option value="orders">{copy.deptOptions.orders}</option>
      <option value="quality">{copy.deptOptions.quality}</option>
      <option value="hr">{copy.deptOptions.hr}</option>
      <option value="press">{copy.deptOptions.press}</option>
      </select>
      </div>

      <div>
      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
      {copy.msgLabel}
      </label>
      <textarea
      rows={4}
      required
      value={message}
      onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
      className="w-full p-3 text-base sm:text-sm border border-slate-200 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8]"
      />
      </div>

      <label className="flex items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-600 cursor-pointer select-none">
      <input
      type="checkbox"
      required
      checked={consent}
      onChange={(e) => setConsent(e.target.checked)}
      className="mt-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-[#0057B8] focus:ring-[#0057B8]"
      />
      <span>{copy.consentLabel}</span>
      </label>

      <button
      type="submit"
      className="w-full sm:w-auto px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
      >
      {copy.submit}
      </button>
      </form>
    )}
    </div>
    </section>
    </div>
  );
};

          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
          {mainPkg.unitsPerBox} {copy.boxUnit} · {localize(mainPkg.packagingType)}
          </div>
        )}
        </td>
        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">
        {mainPkg ? (
          <>
          <div className="font-medium">{localize(mainPkg.shelfLife)}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{localize(mainPkg.storage)}</div>
          </>
        ) : (
          '—'
        )}
        </td>
        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => downloadProductB2BSpec(prod, cat, language)}
        title={t('downloadSpecBtn')}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
        >
        <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>{copy.specBtn}</span>
        </button>
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => {
          setSelectedProduct(prod);
          setModalOpen(true);
        }}
        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
        >
        {copy.quoteBtn}
        </button>
        </div>
        </td>
        </tr>
      );
    })}
    </tbody>
    </table>
    </div>
    </section>

    {/* 4. 4-Step Export Process */}
    <section className="space-y-5">
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.stepsTitle}</h2>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
    {copy.steps.map((step) => (
      <div key={step.num} className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-5 bg-slate-50/70 space-y-2">
      <div className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-[#0057B8]">{step.num}</div>
      <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{step.title}</h3>
      <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{step.desc}</p>
      </div>
    ))}
    </div>
    </section>

    {/* 5. Quality & Compliance */}
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
    {copy.compliance.map((item, idx) => {
      const Icon = complianceIcons[idx];
      return (
        <div key={item.title} className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2.5 bg-white">
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0057B8]" />
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{item.title}</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{item.desc}</p>
        </div>
      );
    })}
    </section>

    <B2BInquiryModal
    open={modalOpen}
    onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
    product={selectedProduct}
    defaultChannel="export"
    />
    </div>
  );
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/Home.tsx`:
```
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowRight, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { ASSETS } from '../data/assets';
import { NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS } from '../data/news';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';
import { ProductGrid } from '../components/ProductGrid';
import { SmartFinder } from '../components/SmartFinder';
import { B2BInquiryModal } from '../components/B2BInquiryModal';
import { CategoryIcon } from '../components/CategoryIcon';
import { HomeHeroSection } from '../components/home/HomeHeroSection';
import { HomeAboutSection } from '../components/home/HomeAboutSection';
import { HomeTeamSlider } from '../components/home/HomeTeamSlider';
import { HomeLocationsSection } from '../components/home/HomeLocationsSection';
import { Product, PromoBannerTheme } from '../types';

const FLAGSHIP_ORDER = ['mozzarella-pizza', 'goroden-aged', 'milk-lactose-free', 'butter-825'];

export const Home: React.FC = () => {
  const { t, language, localize } = useLanguage();
  const { products, categories, news, siteSettings } = useCatalogStore();
  const [activeCategoryId, setActiveCategoryId] = useState<string>('all');
  const [inquiryOpen, setInquiryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [inquiryChannel, setInquiryChannel] = useState<'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general'>(
    'wholesale'
  );

  const visibleProducts = useMemo(() => products.filter((p) => p.visible), [products]);

  const displayedProducts = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeCategoryId === 'all') {
      const featuredList = visibleProducts.filter((p) => p.featured);
      const customFeatured = featuredList.filter(
        (p) => !FLAGSHIP_ORDER.includes(p.id) && p.id.startsWith('custom-')
      );
      const prioritized = FLAGSHIP_ORDER.map((id) => featuredList.find((p) => p.id === id)).filter(
        (p): p is Product => Boolean(p)
      );
      const restFeatured = featuredList.filter(
        (p) => !FLAGSHIP_ORDER.includes(p.id) && !p.id.startsWith('custom-')
      );
      return [...customFeatured, ...prioritized, ...restFeatured].slice(0, 4);
    }
    return visibleProducts.filter((p) => p.category === activeCategoryId).slice(0, 4);
  }, [visibleProducts, activeCategoryId]);

  const activeCategory = categories.find((c) => c.id === activeCategoryId);
  const latestNews = news.filter((n) => n.published).slice(0, 3);
  const aboutImageSrc = latestNews[0]?.image || ASSETS.heroDairyShowcase;

  const { homeHero, promoBanner } = siteSettings;
  const seoTitle = localize(homeHero.seoTitle);
  const seoDesc = localize(homeHero.seoDescription) || localize(homeHero.subtitle);

  const allTabLabel = {
    ru: 'Хиты ассортимента',
    be: 'Хіты асартыменту',
    en: 'Top Highlights',
    zh: '精选热销',
  }[language];

  const primaryPromoLabel = activeCategory
  ? `${localize(promoBanner.primaryCtaLabel) || t('allProductsLink')}: ${localize(activeCategory.name)}`
  : localize(promoBanner.primaryCtaLabel) || t('allProductsLink');

  const primaryPromoTo = activeCategory
  ? `/catalog/${activeCategory.slug}`
  : promoBanner.primaryCtaTo || '/catalog';

  const bannerThemeClasses: Record<
  PromoBannerTheme,
  {
    container: string;
    eyebrow: string;
    heading: string;
    lead: string;
    secBtn: string;
    primBtn: string;
  }
  > = {
    brand: {
      container:
      'bg-gradient-to-r from-[#0057B8] via-[#0a65c2] to-[#0284c7] text-white shadow-sm',
      eyebrow: 'text-blue-100',
      heading: 'text-white',
      lead: 'text-blue-50/95',
      secBtn: 'bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 border border-white/25 text-white',
      primBtn: 'bg-white text-[#0057B8] hover:bg-blue-50 shadow-xs',
    },
    slate: {
      container:
      'bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-slate-800 to-slate-700 text-white shadow-sm',
      eyebrow: 'text-slate-300',
      heading: 'text-white',
      lead: 'text-slate-200',
      secBtn: 'bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 border border-white/20 text-white',
      primBtn: 'bg-white text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 shadow-xs',
    },
    light: {
      container: 'bg-blue-50/75 border border-blue-200 text-slate-900 shadow-xs',
      eyebrow: 'text-[#0057B8]',
      heading: 'text-slate-900',
      lead: 'text-slate-600',
      secBtn: 'bg-white hover:bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 text-[#0057B8]',
      primBtn: 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white hover:bg-[#004494] shadow-xs',
    },
  };

  const activeTheme = bannerThemeClasses[promoBanner.theme || 'brand'];

  const handleOpenInquiry = (channel: 'export' | 'wholesale' | 'general') => {
    setInquiryChannel(channel);
    setInquiryOpen(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-12 sm:space-y-20">
    <SeoHead title={seoTitle} description={seoDesc} canonicalHash="/" />

    {/* 1. Full-bleed Hero + Live Smart Search */}
    <HomeHeroSection />

    {/* 2. Promotional Product Plaque (Toggleable & Editable in CMS) + Category Filter Tabs + Smart Finder */}
    <section className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-4 sm:space-y-5">
    {promoBanner.enabled && (
      <div
      className={`rounded-2xl p-4 sm:px-6 sm:py-4.5 flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3.5 ${activeTheme.container}`}
      >
      <div className="space-y-1 min-w-0">
      <div
      className={`flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider ${activeTheme.eyebrow}`}
      >
      {localize(promoBanner.badge) && (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
        <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
        {localize(promoBanner.badge)}
        </span>
      )}
      {localize(promoBanner.badge) && localize(promoBanner.extra) && (
        <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      )}
      {localize(promoBanner.extra) && <span>{localize(promoBanner.extra)}</span>}
      </div>
      <h2
      className={`font-display text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${activeTheme.heading}`}
      >
      {localize(promoBanner.title) || t('featuredHeading')}
      </h2>
      {localize(promoBanner.lead) && (
        <p className={`text-xs sm:text-sm max-w-2xl leading-snug ${activeTheme.lead}`}>
        {localize(promoBanner.lead)}
        </p>
      )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
      {localize(promoBanner.secondaryCtaLabel) && (
        <Link
        to={promoBanner.secondaryCtaTo || '/catalog/cheese'}
        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${activeTheme.secBtn}`}
        >
        <span>{localize(promoBanner.secondaryCtaLabel)}</span>
        </Link>
      )}
      <Link
      to={primaryPromoTo}
      className={`group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${activeTheme.primBtn}`}
      >
      <span>{primaryPromoLabel}</span>
      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
      </Link>
      </div>
      </div>
    )}

    {/* Category pills */}
    <div className="flex flex-nowrap sm:flex-wrap gap-2 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar -mx-4 px-4 scroll-px-4 sm:mx-0 sm:px-0 sm:scroll-px-0 pb-1 sm:pb-0">
    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={() => setActiveCategoryId('all')}
    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg border transition-all duration-200 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
      activeCategoryId === 'all'
      ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
      : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
    }`}
    >
    <CategoryIcon categoryId="all" className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
    <span>{allTabLabel}</span>
    </button>
    {categories.map((cat) => {
      const active = activeCategoryId === cat.id;
      return (
        <button
        key={cat.id}
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setActiveCategoryId(cat.id)}
        className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg border transition-all duration-200 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0 ${
          active
          ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
          : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
        }`}
        >
        <CategoryIcon categoryId={cat.id} className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
        <span>{localize(cat.name)}</span>
        </button>
      );
    })}
    </div>

    <ProductGrid
    products={displayedProducts}
    mobileCarousel
    variant="promo"
    desktopCols={4}
    scrollResetKey={activeCategoryId}
    />

    <SmartFinder />
    </section>

    {/* 3. Enterprise Story & Production Metrics */}
    <HomeAboutSection imageSrc={aboutImageSrc} />

    {/* 4. Interactive B2B, Quality & Export Specialists Slider */}
    <HomeTeamSlider onRequestContact={handleOpenInquiry} />

    {/* 5. Interactive Production Sites & Cold-Chain Map Tabs */}
    <HomeLocationsSection />

    {/* 6. Latest Newsroom */}
    <section className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 space-y-6">
    <div className="flex items-end justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{t('navNews')}</h2>
    <Link
    to="/news"
    className="group inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap shrink-0"
    >
    <span>
    {language === 'ru'
      ? 'Все новости'
      : language === 'be'
      ? 'Усе навіны'
      : language === 'zh'
      ? '全部新闻'
  : 'All news'}
  </span>
  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
  </Link>
  </div>

  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
  {latestNews.map((item) => (
    <Link
    key={item.id}
    to={`/news/${item.slug}`}
    className="group bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 ease-out"
    >
    <div className="aspect-[16/10] bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-100">
    <ImageWithFallback
    src={item.image}
    alt={localize(item.title)}
    className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.04] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
    />
    </div>
    <div className="p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between space-y-3">
    <div className="space-y-2">
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="font-mono">{item.date}</span>
    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-medium">
    {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[item.categoryId])}
    </span>
    </div>
    <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors leading-snug">
    {localize(item.title)}
    </h3>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
    {localize(item.excerpt)}
    </p>
    </div>
    </div>
    </Link>
  ))}
  </div>
  </section>

  <B2BInquiryModal
  open={inquiryOpen}
  onClose={() => setInquiryOpen(false)}
  defaultChannel={inquiryChannel}
  />
  </div>
  );
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/NewsArticle.tsx`:
```
import React from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS } from '../data/news';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';

export const NewsArticle: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { language, localize, t } = useLanguage();
  const { news } = useCatalogStore();

  const article = news.find((n) => n.slug === id || n.id === id);

  const notFoundCopy = {
    ru: { seoTitle: 'Материал не найден', seoDesc: 'Новость не найдена.', title: 'Публикация не найдена' },
    be: { seoTitle: 'Матэрыял не знойдзены', seoDesc: 'Навіна не знойдзена.', title: 'Публікацыя не знойдзена' },
    en: { seoTitle: 'Article not found', seoDesc: 'Requested article was not found.', title: 'Article not found' },
    zh: { seoTitle: '未找到文章', seoDesc: '未找到相关新闻报道。', title: '未找到该新闻报道' },
  }[language];

  if (!article) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-16 text-center space-y-4">
      <SeoHead title={`${notFoundCopy.seoTitle} — ${t('brandName')}`} description={notFoundCopy.seoDesc} />
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{notFoundCopy.title}</h1>
      <Link to="/news" className="inline-flex px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg">
      {t('navNews')}
      </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const related = news.filter((n) => n.published && article.relatedIds.includes(n.id));

  return (
    <article className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-12 space-y-8">
    <SeoHead
    title={localize(article.seo.title)}
    description={localize(article.seo.description)}
    canonicalHash={`/news/${article.slug}`}
    />

    <div className="space-y-4">
    <Link to="/news" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-[#0057B8]">
    <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    <span>{t('navNews')}</span>
    </Link>

    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
    <span className="font-mono">{article.date}</span>
    <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-medium">
    {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[article.categoryId])}
    </span>
    </div>

    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 leading-tight">
    {localize(article.title)}
    </h1>

    <p className="text-base sm:text-lg text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{localize(article.excerpt)}</p>
    </div>

    <div className="aspect-[16/9] rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50">
    <ImageWithFallback src={article.image} alt={localize(article.title)} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
    </div>

    <div className="text-base text-slate-800 leading-relaxed space-y-4 max-w-[70ch]">
    <p>{localize(article.content)}</p>
    </div>

    {related.length > 0 && (
      <section className="pt-8 border-t border-slate-200 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {language === 'ru'
        ? 'Связанные материалы'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Звязаныя матэрыялы'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '相关新闻'
    : 'Related News'}
    </h2>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
    {related.map((item) => (
      <Link
      key={item.id}
      to={`/news/${item.slug}`}
      className="p-4 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 transition-colors space-y-1.5"
      >
      <div className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-500 tabular-nums">{item.date}</div>
      <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-[#0057B8]">{localize(item.title)}</div>
      </Link>
    ))}
    </div>
    </section>
    )}
    </article>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/News.tsx`:
```
import React, { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Search, X, ArrowRight, Calendar, Newspaper } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS } from '../data/news';
import { NewsCategoryId } from '../types';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';

const NEWS_CATEGORIES: {
  id: 'all' | NewsCategoryId;
  label: Record<'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh', string>;
}[] = [
  { id: 'all', label: { ru: 'Все рубрики', be: 'Усе рубрыкі', en: 'All topics', zh: '全部栏目' } },
{ id: 'export', label: NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS.export },
{ id: 'products', label: NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS.products },
{ id: 'company', label: NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS.company },
{ id: 'awards', label: NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS.awards },
];

export const News: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, localize, t } = useLanguage();
  const { news } = useCatalogStore();

  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<'all' | NewsCategoryId>('all');
  const [selectedYear, setSelectedYear] = useState<string>('all');

  const copy = {
    ru: {
      eyebrow: 'Пресс-центр',
      seoDesc:
      'Новости ОАО «Молочный Мир»: модернизация производства, экспортные контракты, новинки ассортимента и отраслевые награды.',
      lead: 'Архив корпоративных публикаций, экспортных событий и запусков новых производственных линий.',
      searchPlaceholder: 'Поиск по новостям…',
      allYears: 'Все годы',
      latestBadge: 'Последняя новость',
      readFull: 'Читать полностью',
      readMore: 'Читать далее',
      archiveTitle: 'Все публикации',
      foundCount: (n: number) => `Публикаций: ${n}`,
      emptyTitle: 'Публикации не найдены',
      empty: 'По выбранным параметрам и поисковому запросу новости не найдены.',
      reset: 'Сбросить фильтры',
    },
    be: {
      eyebrow: 'Прэс-цэнтр',
      seoDesc:
      'Навіны ААТ «Малочны Мір»: мадэрнізацыя вытворчасці, экспартныя кантракты, навінкі і ўзнагароды.',
      lead: 'Архіў карпаратыўных публікацый, экспартных падзей і запускаў новых вытворчых ліній.',
      searchPlaceholder: 'Пошук па навінах…',
      allYears: 'Усе гады',
      latestBadge: 'Апошняя навіна',
      readFull: 'Чытаць цалкам',
      readMore: 'Чытаць далей',
      archiveTitle: 'Усе публікацыі',
      foundCount: (n: number) => `Публікацый: ${n}`,
      emptyTitle: 'Публікацыі не знойдзены',
      empty: 'Па выбраных параметрах і пошукавым запыце навіны не знойдзены.',
      reset: 'Скінуць фільтры',
    },
    en: {
      eyebrow: 'Newsroom',
      seoDesc: 'Latest production, export, and corporate news from Molochny Mir.',
      lead: 'Corporate announcements, export milestones, and product line launches.',
      searchPlaceholder: 'Search newsroom…',
      allYears: 'All years',
      latestBadge: 'Latest News',
      readFull: 'Read full story',
      readMore: 'Read more',
      archiveTitle: 'More Stories',
      foundCount: (n: number) => `Articles: ${n}`,
      emptyTitle: 'No articles found',
      empty: 'No news items match the selected category, year, or search query.',
      reset: 'Reset filters',
    },
    zh: {
      eyebrow: '新闻中心',
      seoDesc: 'Molochny Mir 企业新闻：产线升级、国际出口合约、新品发布与行业荣誉。',
      lead: '企业新闻、新产线投产与全球出口动态存档。',
      searchPlaceholder: '搜索新闻标题或内容…',
      allYears: '全部年份',
      latestBadge: '最新动态',
      readFull: '阅读全文',
      readMore: '查看详情',
      archiveTitle: '更多新闻报道',
      foundCount: (n: number) => `新闻数: ${n}`,
      emptyTitle: '未找到相关新闻',
      empty: '没有符合当前筛选条件的新闻报道。',
      reset: '重置筛选',
    },
  }[language];

  const publishedSorted = useMemo(
    () => news.filter((n) => n.published).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date)),
                                  [news]
  );

  const newestArticleId = publishedSorted[0]?.id;

  const years = useMemo(() => {
    const set = new Set<number>(publishedSorted.map((n) => n.year));
    return Array.from(set).sort((a, b) => b - a);
  }, [publishedSorted]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    return publishedSorted.filter((n) => {
      if (selectedCategory !== 'all' && n.categoryId !== selectedCategory) return false;
      if (selectedYear !== 'all' && String(n.year) !== selectedYear) return false;
      if (q) {
        const text = [
          n.title.ru,
          n.title.be,
          n.title.en,
          n.title.zh,
          n.excerpt.ru,
          n.excerpt.be,
          n.excerpt.en,
          n.excerpt.zh,
          n.content.ru,
          n.content.be,
          n.content.en,
          n.content.zh,
        ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' ')
        .toLowerCase();
        if (!text.includes(q)) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [publishedSorted, query, selectedCategory, selectedYear]);

  const hasActiveFilters =
  query.trim().length > 0 || selectedCategory !== 'all' || selectedYear !== 'all';

  const handleReset = () => {
    setQuery('');
    setSelectedCategory('all');
    setSelectedYear('all');
  };

  const featuredArticle = filtered[0];
  const restArticles = filtered.slice(1);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-12 space-y-8">
    <SeoHead
    title={`${t('navNews')} — ${t('brandName')} (milk.by)`}
    description={copy.seoDesc}
    canonicalHash="/news"
    />

    {/* Заголовок и счётчик */}
    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-end justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
    <div className="space-y-2.5 max-w-2xl">
    <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#0057B8]">
    {copy.eyebrow}
    </p>
    <h1 className="font-display text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">
    {t('navNews')}
    </h1>
    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{copy.lead}</p>
    </div>
    <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 tabular-nums shrink-0">
    {copy.foundCount(filtered.length)}
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Панель поиска и фильтров */}
    <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-4">
    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">
    <div className="relative flex-1">
    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" />
    <input
    type="text"
    value={query}
    onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
    placeholder={copy.searchPlaceholder}
    className="w-full h-10 pl-10 pr-9 text-base sm:text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0057B8]/15 transition-all"
    />
    {query && (
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setQuery('')}
      aria-label="Clear search"
      className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-7 h-7 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
      >
      <X className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    )}
    </div>

    <select
    value={selectedYear}
    onChange={(e) => setSelectedYear(e.target.value)}
    aria-label="Filter by year"
    className="h-10 px-3.5 text-base sm:text-sm bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#0057B8] tabular-nums"
    >
    <option value="all">{copy.allYears}</option>
    {years.map((y) => (
      <option key={y} value={String(y)}>
      {y}
      </option>
    ))}
    </select>
    </div>

    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
    {NEWS_CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
      <button
      key={cat.id}
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setSelectedCategory(cat.id)}
      className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg border transition-colors whitespace-nowrap ${
        selectedCategory === cat.id
        ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
        : 'bg-white text-slate-700 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
      }`}
      >
      {localize(cat.label)}
      </button>
    ))}
    </div>

    {hasActiveFilters && (
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={handleReset}
      className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#0057B8] hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
      >
      {copy.reset}
      </button>
    )}
    </div>
    </div>

    {/* Пустое состояние */}
    {filtered.length === 0 ? (
      <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50/70 p-12 text-center space-y-3">
      <Newspaper className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-400 mx-auto" strokeWidth={1.5} />
      <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">{copy.emptyTitle}</h2>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 max-w-md mx-auto">{copy.empty}</p>
      {hasActiveFilters && (
        <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleReset}
        className="inline-flex items-center px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
        >
        {copy.reset}
        </button>
      )}
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div className="space-y-10">
      {/* Выделенная последняя / главная новость */}
      {featuredArticle && (
        <Link
        to={`/news/${featuredArticle.slug}`}
        className="group grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-xs hover:border-[#0057B8]/45 hover:shadow-xl transition-all duration-200"
        >
        <div className="lg:col-span-7 relative aspect-[16/10] lg:aspect-auto lg:min-h-[360px] bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden">
        <ImageWithFallback
        src={featuredArticle.image}
        alt={localize(featuredArticle.title)}
        className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.03] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
        />
        {featuredArticle.id === newestArticleId && (
          <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#0057B8] text-white text-xs font-semibold shadow-md">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-white animate-pulse motion-reduce:animate-none" />
          <span>{copy.latestBadge}</span>
          </div>
        )}
        </div>

        <div className="lg:col-span-5 p-6 sm:p-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-slate-50/60">
        <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5 text-xs tabular-nums">
        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-semibold border border-blue-100">
        {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[featuredArticle.categoryId])}
        </span>
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-slate-500">
        <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
        {featuredArticle.date}
        </span>
        </div>

        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-[1.7rem] font-bold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors leading-snug">
        {localize(featuredArticle.title)}
        </h2>

        <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed line-clamp-4">
        {localize(featuredArticle.excerpt)}
        </p>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-[#0057B8] group-hover:text-[#004494] transition-colors">
        <span>{copy.readFull}</span>
        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
        </span>
        </div>
        </div>
        </Link>
      )}

      {/* Остальные новости */}
      {restArticles.length > 0 && (
        <section className="space-y-5">
        <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {copy.archiveTitle}
        </h2>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {restArticles.map((article) => (
          <Link
          key={article.id}
          to={`/news/${article.slug}`}
          className="group bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 ease-out"
          >
          <div className="aspect-[16/10] bg-slate-50 overflow-hidden border-b border-slate-100">
          <ImageWithFallback
          src={article.image}
          alt={localize(article.title)}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-[1.04] transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
          />
          </div>
          <div className="p-5 flex-1 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 tabular-nums">
          <span className="font-mono">{article.date}</span>
          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-medium">
          {localize(NEWS_CATEGORY_LABELS[article.categoryId])}
          </span>
          </div>
          <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors leading-snug">
          {localize(article.title)}
          </h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
          {localize(article.excerpt)}
          </p>
          </div>

          <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 group-hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
          <span>{copy.readMore}</span>
          <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-200 group-hover:translate-x-1" />
          </div>
          </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
        </div>
        </section>
      )}
      </div>
    )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

        to={`/product/${prod.slug}`}
        className="inline-flex items-start gap-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-[#0057B8] leading-snug"

        {prod.fatContent !== undefined ? `${prod.fatContent}% · ` : ''}
        {prod.packages.map((p) => p.weight).join(' / ')}
        </div>
        </div>
        </div>

        type="button"
        onClick={() => downloadProductB2BSpec(prod, cat, language)}
        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"

        type="button"

        }}

        >
        {copy.quoteBtn}
        </button>
        </div>
        </div>
      );
    })}
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>
    </section>

    {/* 3. 4-Step B2B Onboarding */}
    <section className="space-y-5">
    <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.stepsTitle}</h2>
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5">
    {copy.steps.map((s) => (
      <div
      key={s.num}
      className="border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2.5 bg-white hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 transition-colors"
      >

      <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{s.title}</h3>
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{s.desc}</p>
      </div>
    ))}
    </div>
    </section>

    {/* 4. Operational Pillars */}
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
    {copy.features.map((feat, idx) => {
      const Icon = featureIcons[idx];
      return (
        <div
        key={feat.title}
        className="bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 space-y-2.5"
        >
        <Icon className="w-5 h-5 text-[#0057B8]" />
        <h3 className="font-display text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{feat.title}</h3>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">{feat.desc}</p>
        </div>
      );
    })}
    </section>

    <B2BInquiryModal
    open={modalOpen}
    onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
    product={selectedProduct}
    defaultChannel="wholesale"
    />
    </div>
  );
};

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/Product.tsx`:
```
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Download,
  MessageSquare,
  Package,
  Thermometer,
  Clock,
  Layers,
  ShieldCheck,
  Check,
  Globe2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { ImageWithFallback } from '../components/ImageWithFallback';
import { ProductGrid } from '../components/ProductGrid';
import { B2BInquiryModal } from '../components/B2BInquiryModal';
import { downloadProductB2BSpec } from '../utils/specExport';

export const Product: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const { language, localize, t } = useLanguage();
  const { products, categories } = useCatalogStore();

  const product = products.find((p) => p.slug === id || p.id === id);
  const [selectedPkgId, setSelectedPkgId] = useState<string>('');
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (product && product.packages.length > 0) {
      setSelectedPkgId((prev) =>
      product.packages.some((pkg) => pkg.id === prev) ? prev : product.packages[0].id
      );
    }
  }, [product]);

  if (!product) {
    return (
      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-16 text-center space-y-4">
      <SeoHead
      title={`${
        language === 'ru'
        ? 'Товар не найден'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Тавар не знойдзены'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未找到产品'
        : 'Product not found'
      } — ${t('brandName')}`}
      description={
        language === 'ru'
        ? 'Запрошенный продукт не найден в каталоге.'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Запытаны прадукт не знойдзены ў каталогу.'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未在目录中找到所请求的产品。'
        : 'Requested product was not found in the catalog.'
      }
      />
      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {language === 'ru'
        ? 'Продукт не найден'
        : language === 'be'
        ? 'Прадукт не знойдзены'
        : language === 'zh'
        ? '未找到该产品'
    : 'Product not found'}
    </h1>
    <Link
    to="/catalog"
    className="inline-flex px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors"
    >
    {t('heroPrimaryCta')}
    </Link>
    </div>
    );
  }

  const category = categories.find((c) => c.id === product.category);
  const activePackage =
  product.packages.find((pkg) => pkg.id === selectedPkgId) || product.packages[0];

  const relatedProducts = products.filter(
    (p) => p.visible && product.relatedIds.includes(p.id)
  );

  const gramUnit = language === 'ru' || language === 'be' ? 'г' : language === 'zh' ? '克' : 'g';

  const labels = {
    ru: {
      home: 'Главная',
      packagesTitle: 'Выберите вариант фасовки',
      weight: 'Масса / объём',
      shelfLife: 'Срок годности',
      storage: 'Условия хранения',
      packagingType: 'Тип упаковки',
      unitsPerBox: 'В тарном месте',
      specsTitle: 'Характеристики продукта',
      specsSubtitle: 'Технические параметры и состав продукции',
      allPacksTitle: 'Все фасовки и логистика короба',
      allPacksSubtitle: 'Нажмите на вариант, чтобы выбрать его для запроса КП',
      fat: 'Массовая доля жира',
      fatShort: 'Жирность',
      protein: 'Белок на 100 г',
      proteinShort: 'Белок / 100 г',
      lactose: 'Лактоза',
      lactoseFreeVal: 'Без лактозы (< 0,01%)',
      lactoseNaturalVal: 'Природная молочная',
      lactoseFreeShort: 'Без лактозы',
      lactoseShort: 'Природная',
      qualityLabel: 'Стандарт',
      exportBadge: 'Экспорт B2B',
      domesticBadge: 'Розница / РБ',
      boxUnitsShort: 'шт/кор',
      related: 'Связанные позиции каталога',
    },
    be: {
      home: 'Галоўная',
      packagesTitle: 'Выберыце варыянт фасоўкі',
      weight: 'Маса / аб’ём',
      shelfLife: 'Тэрмін прыдатнасці',
      storage: 'Умовы захоўвання',
      packagingType: 'Тып упакоўкі',
      unitsPerBox: 'У тарным месцы',
      specsTitle: 'Характарыстыкі прадукту',

      fatShort: '乳脂',

      proteinShort: '蛋白质 / 100克',

      lactoseNaturalVal: '含天然乳糖',
      lactoseFreeShort: '无乳糖',
      lactoseShort: '含天然乳糖',
      qualityLabel: '认证标准',
      exportBadge: '支持出口 B2B',

      related: '相关推荐产品',
    },
  }[language];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10 space-y-12 sm:space-y-14">
    <SeoHead
    title={localize(product.seo.title)}
    description={localize(product.seo.description)}
    canonicalHash={`/product/${product.slug}`}
    />

    {/* Breadcrumbs */}
    <nav className="text-xs text-slate-500 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2" aria-label="Breadcrumb">
    <Link to="/" className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
    {labels.home}
    </Link>
    <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    <Link to="/catalog" className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
    {t('navCatalog')}
    </Link>
    {category && (
      <>
      <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
      <Link to={`/catalog/${category.slug}`} className="hover:text-[#0057B8] transition-colors">
      {localize(category.name)}
      </Link>
      </>
    )}
    <span aria-hidden="true">/</span>
    <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{localize(product.name)}</span>
    </nav>

    {/* Hero Product Overview: Anchored Image Left + Pack Selector & CTAs Right */}
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 lg:gap-10 items-start">
    {/* Left Column: Static Image + Key Nutrition/Compliance Metrics */}
    <div className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
    <div className="aspect-[4/3] w-full rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 shadow-xs">
    <ImageWithFallback
    src={product.image}
    alt={localize(product.name)}
    fallbackTitle={localize(product.name)}
    className="w-full h-full object-cover"
    />
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5 tabular-nums">
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
    <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.fatShort}</div>
    <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
    {product.fatContent !== undefined ? `${product.fatContent}%` : '—'}
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
    <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.proteinShort}</div>
    <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">
    {product.proteinContent !== undefined
      ? `${product.proteinContent} ${gramUnit}`
      : '—'}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.lactose}</div>
      <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 truncate">
      {product.lactoseFree ? labels.lactoseFreeShort : labels.lactoseShort}
      </div>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3.5 py-2.5">
      <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{labels.qualityLabel}</div>
      <div className="font-display text-sm font-bold text-[#0057B8] mt-0.5 truncate">
      FSSC 22000
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
      {category && (
        <Link
        to={`/catalog/${category.slug}`}
        className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0057B8] font-semibold hover:bg-blue-100 transition-colors"
        >
        {localize(category.name)}
        </Link>
      )}
      {product.exportReady ? (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 font-medium border border-emerald-200/70">
        <Globe2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        {labels.exportBadge}
        </span>
      ) : (
        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-medium">
        {labels.domesticBadge}
        </span>
      )}
      {product.lactoseFree && (
        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 font-medium border border-amber-200/70">
        <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        {labels.lactoseFreeShort}
        </span>
      )}
      </div>

      <h1 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight leading-tight">
      {localize(product.seo.h1) || localize(product.name)}
      </h1>

      <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
      {localize(product.description)}
      </p>
      </div>

      {/* Multi-Packaging Variant Selector */}
      {product.packages.length > 0 && (
        <div className="space-y-3 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700">
        {labels.packagesTitle}
        </span>
        <span className="text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums">
        {product.packages.length} SKU
        </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {product.packages.map((pkg) => {
          const isSelected = activePackage?.id === pkg.id;
          return (
            <button
            key={pkg.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedPkgId(pkg.id)}
            aria-pressed={isSelected}
            className={`px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold rounded-xl border transition-all duration-150 tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs'
              : 'bg-white text-slate-800 border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/50 hover:text-[#0057B8]'
            }`}
            >
            {pkg.weight}
            </button>
          );
        })}
        </div>

        {activePackage && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5 bg-slate-50/90 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 text-sm tabular-nums">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Package className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.weight}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{activePackage.weight}</div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.shelfLife}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.shelfLife)}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Thermometer className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.storage}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.storage)}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{labels.unitsPerBox}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold text-slate-900 break-all">
          {activePackage.unitsPerBox} {labels.boxUnitsShort} · GTIN {activePackage.gtin}
          </div>
          </div>
          </div>
          <div className="sm:col-span-2 pt-2.5 border-t border-slate-200/80 text-xs text-slate-600">
          <span className="text-slate-500">{labels.packagingType}: </span>
          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">
          {localize(activePackage.packagingType)}
          </span>
          </div>
          </div>
        )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* B2B Actions */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row flex-wrap gap-3 pt-1">
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)}
      className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-xl hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors shadow-xs"
      >
      <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
      <span>{t('requestSalesBtn')}</span>
      </button>
      <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => downloadProductB2BSpec(product, category, language)}
      className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors"
      >
      <Download className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
      <span>{t('downloadSpecBtn')}</span>
      </button>
      </div>
      </div>
      </div>

      {/* Dedicated Full-Width Section: Technical Specifications & All Pack Formats */}
      <div className="pt-8 border-t border-slate-200 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8 items-start">
      {/* Dynamic Category-Specific Characteristics Table */}
      <section className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
      <div>
      <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
      {labels.specsTitle}
      </h2>
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{labels.specsSubtitle}</p>
      </div>

      <dl className="divide-y divide-slate-200 border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden text-sm tabular-nums bg-white">
      {product.fatContent !== undefined && (
        <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-white">
        <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.fat}</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
        {product.fatContent}%
        </dd>
        </div>
      )}
      {product.proteinContent !== undefined && (
        <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-slate-50/60">
        <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.protein}</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
        {product.proteinContent} {gramUnit}
        </dd>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 bg-white">
      <dt className="text-slate-500">{labels.lactose}</dt>
      <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
      {product.lactoseFree ? labels.lactoseFreeVal : labels.lactoseNaturalVal}
      </dd>
      </div>

      {Object.entries(product.characteristics).map(([key, value], idx) => {
        const schemaItem = category?.characteristicSchema.find((f) => f.key === key);
        const label = schemaItem ? localize(schemaItem.label) : key;
        return (
          <div
          key={key}
          className={`px-4 py-3.5 flex justify-between gap-4 ${
            idx % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-slate-50/60' : 'bg-white'
          }`}
          >
          <dt className="text-slate-500">{label}</dt>
          <dd className="font-semibold text-slate-900 min-w-0 break-words text-right">
          {localize(value)}
          </dd>
          </div>
        );
      })}
      </dl>
      </section>

      {/* Interactive All-Packs Logistics Matrix */}
      {product.packages.length > 0 && (
        <section className="lg:col-span-6 space-y-4">
        <div>
        <h2 className="font-display text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {labels.allPacksTitle}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{labels.allPacksSubtitle}</p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2.5">
        {product.packages.map((pkg) => {
          const isSelected = activePackage?.id === pkg.id;
          return (
            <button
            key={pkg.id}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSelectedPkgId(pkg.id)}
            className={`w-full text-left rounded-2xl border p-4 transition-all duration-150 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 tabular-nums ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-blue-50/70 border-[#0057B8] shadow-xs ring-1 ring-[#0057B8]/20'
              : 'bg-white border-slate-200 hover:border-[#0057B8]/40 hover:bg-slate-50/60'
            }`}
            >
            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-display text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
            {pkg.weight}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 font-medium">
            {localize(pkg.shelfLife)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            {localize(pkg.storage)}
            </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 truncate">
            {localize(pkg.packagingType)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400">
            {pkg.unitsPerBox} {labels.boxUnitsShort} · GTIN {pkg.gtin}
            </div>
            </div>

            <div
            className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-colors ${
              isSelected
              ? 'bg-[#0057B8] text-white'
              : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-400'
            }`}
            >
            <Check className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
        </div>
        </section>
      )}
      </div>

      {/* Related Products */}
      {relatedProducts.length > 0 && (
        <section className="space-y-6 pt-8 border-t border-slate-200">
        <h2 className="font-display text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
        {labels.related}
        </h2>
        <ProductGrid products={relatedProducts} />
        </section>
      )}

      <B2BInquiryModal
      open={modalOpen}
      onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)}
      product={product}
      selectedPackageId={activePackage?.id}
      defaultChannel={product.exportReady ? 'export' : 'wholesale'}
      />
      </div>
  );
};
```

`/home/july/Projects/milkby-hackathon/src/pages/SitemapPage.tsx`:
```
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Download, CheckCircle2, ArrowLeft, LogOut } from 'lucide-react';
import { useLanguage } from '../hooks/useLanguage';
import { useCatalogStore } from '../hooks/useCatalogStore';
import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
import { generateXmlSitemap } from '../utils/specExport';
import { SeoHead } from '../components/SeoHead';
import { StaffAuthGate } from '../components/StaffAuth';

const SitemapPageContent: React.FC = () => {
  const { language, t } = useLanguage();
  const { products, categories, news, redirects } = useCatalogStore();
  const { logout } = useAuth();

  const copy = {

      backToCms: 'Панэль кіравання CMS',
      logout: 'Выйсці',

      noDupes: '(Без дубляў)',
      langVersions: 'Моўныя версіі (hreflang)',
      redirectsCount: 'Наладжана 301-рэдырэктаў',
      rulesUnit: 'правілаў',
      redirectsHeading: 'Карта 301-рэдырэктаў са старых URL milk.by',
      colOld: 'Стары URL (milk.by)',
      colCode: 'Код',
      colNew: 'Новы кананічны URL',
      colNote: 'Заўвага',
    },
    en: {
      seoDesc:
      'Auto-generated sitemap.xml, 4-language hreflang links, and 301 redirect rules from legacy milk.by URLs.',
      h1: 'XML Sitemap, SEO Audit & 301 Redirects',
      lead: 'Auto-generated clean URL sitemap with 4-language hreflang links and legacy 301 redirects.',
      backToCms: 'CMS Panel',
      logout: 'Log out',
      downloadXml: 'Download sitemap.xml',
      uniqTitle: 'Unique SEO Titles',
      noDupes: '(No duplicates)',
      langVersions: 'Language Versions (hreflang)',
      redirectsCount: 'Configured 301 Redirects',
      rulesUnit: 'rules',
      redirectsHeading: 'Legacy URL 301 Redirect Mapping',

      colCode: 'Code',
      colNew: 'Canonical Target URL',

    },
    zh: {
      seoDesc:
      '自动更新的 sitemap.xml、四种语言 hreflang 关联及 milk.by 旧版链接 301 重定向映射表。',
      h1: 'XML 站点地图、SEO 审计与 301 重定向',
      lead: '自动生成支持四种语言 hreflang (RU / BE / EN / ZH) 且无重复标题的规范化 URL 站点地图。',
      backToCms: '返回管理后台',
      logout: '退出登录',
      downloadXml: '下载 sitemap.xml',
      uniqTitle: 'SEO 标题唯一性',
      noDupes: '（无重复）',
      langVersions: '语言版本 (hreflang)',
      redirectsCount: '已配置 301 重定向',
      rulesUnit: '条规则',
      redirectsHeading: 'milk.by 旧版链接 301 重定向映射表',
      colOld: '旧版 URL (milk.by)',
      colCode: '状态码',
      colNew: '新版规范 URL',
      colNote: '说明',
    },
  }[language];

  const xmlContent = useMemo(
    () => generateXmlSitemap(products, categories, news),
                             [products, categories, news]
  );

  const seoAudit = useMemo(() => {
    const visibleProducts = products.filter((p) => p.visible);
    const titles = visibleProducts.map((p) => p.seo.title[language] || p.seo.title.ru);
    const uniqueTitles = new Set(titles);
    return {
      totalProducts: visibleProducts.length,
      uniqueTitlesCount: uniqueTitles.size,
      noDuplicates: titles.length === uniqueTitles.size,
    };
  }, [products, language]);

  const handleDownloadXml = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([xmlContent], { type: 'application/xml;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'sitemap.xml';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-8 sm:py-10 space-y-10">
    <SeoHead
    title={`XML Sitemap & SEO Audit — ${t('brandName')}`}
    description={copy.seoDesc}
    canonicalHash="/sitemap"
    noIndex
    />

    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-5">
    <div>
    <h1 className="font-display text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{copy.h1}</h1>
    <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 mt-1">{copy.lead}</p>
    </div>

    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 self-start sm:self-auto shrink-0">
    <Link
    to="/admin"
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-200 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
    >
    <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    <span>{copy.backToCms}</span>
    </Link>

    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={handleDownloadXml}
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white bg-[#0057B8] rounded-lg hover:bg-[#004494] transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
    >
    <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
    <span>{copy.downloadXml}</span>
    </button>

    <button
    type="button"
    onClick={logout}
    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors whitespace-nowrap"
    >
    <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
    <span>{copy.logout}</span>
    </button>
    </div>
    </div>

    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 tabular-nums">
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.uniqTitle}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#0057B8] shrink-0" />
    <span>
    {seoAudit.uniqueTitlesCount} / {seoAudit.totalProducts}{' '}
    {seoAudit.noDuplicates ? copy.noDupes : ''}
    </span>
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.langVersions}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    4 (RU · BE · EN · ZH)
    </div>
    </div>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl p-4 bg-white">
    <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{copy.redirectsCount}</div>
    <div className="mt-1 font-display text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
    {redirects.length} {copy.rulesUnit}
    </div>
    </div>
    </div>

    <section className="space-y-3">
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    {copy.redirectsHeading}
    </h2>
    <div className="border border-slate-200 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto bg-white">
    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
    <thead>
    <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 font-semibold text-slate-600">
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colOld}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colCode}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colNew}</th>
    <th className="py-3 px-4">{copy.colNote}</th>
    </tr>
    </thead>
    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200">
    {redirects.map((rule) => {
      const targetPath = rule.to.replace(/^\/?#/, '') || '/';
    return (
      <tr key={rule.from}>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600">{rule.from}</td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono font-semibold text-[#0057B8]">
      {rule.code}
      </td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 font-mono">
      <Link to={targetPath} className="text-[#0057B8] hover:underline">
      {rule.to}
      </Link>
      </td>
      <td className="py-2.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{rule.note}</td>
      </tr>
    );
    })}
    </tbody>
    </table>
    </div>
    </section>

    <section className="space-y-3">
    <h2 className="font-display text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
    sitemap.xml (Live Preview)
    </h2>
    <pre className="p-4 bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 rounded-xl overflow-x-auto text-xs font-mono leading-relaxed max-h-[420px]">
    {xmlContent}
    </pre>
    </section>
    </div>
  );
};

export const SitemapPage: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <StaffAuthGate>
    <SitemapPageContent />
    </StaffAuthGate>
  );
};

export type Language = 'ru' | 'be' | 'en' | 'zh';

export type LocalizedText = Record<Language, string>;

export interface PackageVariant {
  id: string;
  weight: string;
  weightGrams: number;
  shelfLife: LocalizedText;
  storage: LocalizedText;
  packagingType: LocalizedText;
  unitsPerBox: number;
  gtin: string;
}

  title: LocalizedText;
  description: LocalizedText;
  h1: LocalizedText;
}

export interface Product {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  category: string;
  subcategory: string;

}
